```sh
  CODE_SIGN_INJECT_BASE_ENTITLEMENTS=NO ARCHS=arm64 ONLY_ACTIVE_ARCH=YES
```

Apple's [current development-profile instructions](https://developer.apple.com/help/account/provisioning-profiles/create-a-development-provisioning-profile/) list **Mac App Development** and confirm that Xcode manages development profiles with automatic signing. That is development qualification, not Developer ID/notarization or a Phase 0 pass.

<a id="provisioned-native-keychain-proof"></a>
#### Provisioned native Keychain proof (2026-10-26)

- **Actual profile:** Xcode issued `Mac Team Provisioning Profile: *`, UUID `7ea64a84-9906-4a93-a766-916e27994786`, Team `476T8NQCSX`, expiring `2027-10-26T12:28:03Z`. Its application identifier and Keychain group authorization are `476T8NQCSX.*`. This is an **Apple-managed wildcard Mac development profile**, not an explicit per-app Developer ID/release profile and not an Apollo profile. The [nonsecret profile/entitlement record](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-provisioning/provisioned-profile-scope.json) excludes device IDs and certificate bytes.
- **Sealed product:** strict/deep code-signature verification passed for the arm64 Release app and its packaged helper, both genuinely Apple Development signed with hardened runtime. The app seals exact identifier `476T8NQCSX.com.mehmetcanbudak.memoshi.mac` and only secret group `476T8NQCSX.com.mehmetcanbudak.memoshi.secrets`; the helper has no secret-group entitlement. No Debug entitlement relaxation or authentication bypass was used.
- **Real native-to-model path:** a memory-only disposable canary was typed into the actual native SecureField with **Keep On This Mac Only** checked; native Save stored it in Apple's data-protection Keychain. A separately signed consumer of the real production Keychain actor verified the value, redacted release, 60-second TTL and wrong-provider refusal. The native app remained the exclusive authenticated broker host; a second genuine public client was UI-only. A loopback proxy enforced that exact Bearer key and forwarded only model traffic to real installed Ollama `qwen3:0.6b`, without forwarding the key upstream or fabricating a response. The real Pi turn returned **`5`**, and replay retained the original receipt and exact durable history. [Actual captured output](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-provisioning/native-keychain-broker-proof.txt), [saved-key settings](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-provisioning/native-provisioned-key-settings.png), [actual answer](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-provisioning/native-keyed-ollama-answer.png).
- **Revocation and privacy:** deletion first verified ownership by comparing the protected value with the in-memory canary, then removed only that reference. Subsequent OS get failed; public discovery reported local unavailable, omitted its stale models and made **zero further HTTP requests**. Raw/base64 canary bytes were absent from both owned app/helper arguments/environment, captured outputs, six retained proof files and all **8 files / 327,744 bytes** in the fresh process home after normal app/helper exit. This is scoped application/store evidence, not a whole-system unified-log or user-home audit.
- **Normal teardown and review:** normal Quit stopped the app and its owned helper without forced signals. The memory-only proxy was stopped; current local configuration was explicitly restored to real keyless Ollama at `http://127.0.0.1:11434/v1`, with no test secret reference. The provisioned copy at `build/ProvisionedKeychain.ryna01i2/Memoshi.app` reopened its actual arithmetic conversation in isolated home `/tmp/mkc20-535xtzgb`; [review settings](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-provisioning/native-provisioned-keyless-review.png) show **No key** and the real model. It is open for review. Disposable probe sources/apps and their own generated consumer binary/intermediates were removed; the installed authorized profile and usable app remain. The user's existing `/tmp/mkcui-lom071ct` conversation, its app, the unrelated prototype root and Pi_Swift were not reused or changed.
- **Still unproven:** screen lock/unlock, iCloud synchronization/other-device access, actual OpenAI/cloud API use, explicit per-app Developer ID profiles/notarization and S5 release checks, held deliberate-kill/physical-device scenarios, a qualifying Pi `slice-N` and complete S1/Phase 0 acceptance. P0-20's device-only native path is proved, not a blanket completion of these gates. Tools, policy, Apple connectors and the iOS engine remain disabled. No production source changed in this provisioning continuation; the preceding **165-test** CI record remains historical, not a rerun or substitute for this real OS/runtime proof.






<a id="policy-approval-development-proof"></a>
### Policy and durable-approval development proof (2026-10-26)

- **Scope:** D90 pre-tag development against unchanged Pi candidate `892585fc8d8be3093b58c34aacffcd4e68c3a8d9`. The sibling's working tree was not changed. This implements a bounded part of P0-06, not its S1 acceptance or permission to enable production tools.
- **Runtime:** each owned Chat installs mandatory `pi.tool` before/after hooks. Trusted tool metadata drives the T0–T5/mode/origin matrix; current stored mode/hard stops and the trusted host's required grant/target guard are checked again at dispatch. The pinned SDK passes `TaskRuntime` through `HookApi`; a checked cast obtains real `TaskRuntime.memo`, not an invented memo table. `memoshi.approval` binds one durable Pi task to one decision/card; a separate durable authorization memo makes missing-hook/recovery dispatch fail closed. T4/T5 tool definitions are rejected in this profile.
- **Store and protocol:** schema 4 persists decision bindings, current policy and sticky exposure. Approval/card/task-wait changes and their facts commit together. The first valid user answer wins; same-request replay returns the original receipt, exact-deadline answers commit expiry, cancellation prevents late resurrection, and startup expires/cancels orphaned pending decisions. `cards.list/get/respond` are real signed-app RPCs, not a generic record mutation. `allow_task`/`always_allow`, grant creation, notification answers and device-owner proof verification are not implemented here.
- **Output and audit:** both model/user result forms and metadata are scrubbed before Pi may commit them; an internal scrub failure returns withheld output rather than throwing through Pi's raw-result fallback. Class-A envelopes are mirrored atomically into a separate local table from schema 4 onward. It is **not** the required HMAC chain/peer-anchor system and does not backfill historical A events.
- **Native review:** one authoritative approval/card projection serves conversation cards, Waiting for You, related-call groups and its pending badge. Paginated snapshots/event application precede receipt acknowledgment; only opaque original response IDs/actions persist for uncertain delivery. Native buttons offer Allow once/Deny, disable expired/inconsistent/terminal decisions and do not optimistically claim execution. Current/Warm and the separate design examples remain. Since 2026-11-20 the projection reloads its snapshot by itself once when a reconnect leaves it stale, and a pending Card says why its buttons are disabled while review is not synchronized ([round 2 fixes](#fixes-2026-11-20-round2)). Since 2026-11-21 the iPhone Card's buttons sit side by side when they fit and one per line otherwise, through DesignSystem's `DesignFittingStack` instead of `ViewThatFits` ([work of 2026-11-21](#work-2026-11-21)).
- **Automated proof:** [complete `scripts/ci.sh` output](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-policy-approvals/final-checks.txt) passed over **205 Swift files**, **176 tests across six packages**, **138 golden fixtures**, 31 accepted dependency occurrences, schema drift, Mac/universal iOS Simulator builds and bundle/notices checks. Real pinned-Pi contracts exercise actual file effects, denial, grant revocation during a prompt, normal-close cancellation, nested authorization, original-receipt replay/expiry and released-secret raw/encoded forms in both outputs and the next HTTP model request. Incidental migration-version pins were removed, not repinned; migration backup/rollback/newer-store behavior remains tested.
- **Actual signed native proof:** an isolated trusted-host helper registered one test tool for two private files; only its external HTTP provider was controlled. The real app, same-FD authentication, Pi, SQLite and native review ran unchanged. [Pending review](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-policy-approvals/native-waiting-for-you.png) showed one pending decision and no file. Native Allow once created the approved file; native Deny left the separate file absent. Both deciding actors are `user`. Actual A-event/mirror counts matched: requested 2/2, resolved 2/2, policy 3/3 and tool completions 2/2. [Observed state/effects/signing](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-policy-approvals/verification.json) and the [provider effect trace](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-policy-approvals/provider.jsonl) retain the limits.
- **Normal cutback and review:** normal Quit ended the fixture; its provider, source, compiled helper and grant marker were removed. The original normal helper was restored byte-for-byte and the copied app re-sealed with the existing authorized Apple Development identity/profile, without a helper secret group or auth bypass. After normal reopen, [both recorded decisions loaded](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-policy-approvals/native-reopened-normal.png), with zero pending and no terminal actions. Real keyless `local/qwen3:0.6b` at `http://127.0.0.1:11434/v1` then answered **5** to **2 + 3** in the normal no-tools app ([actual capture](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-policy-approvals/native-real-model.png)). Current review: `build/PolicyApproval.743gzoo5/Memoshi.app`, isolated home `/tmp/mpolicy-vljh4tw2`, on Waiting for You. Earlier review homes/apps and unrelated prototype data were preserved.
- **Qualification limits and next work:** keyboard automation did not deliver characters even to a focused composer, so native shortcut/focus behavior remains **unqualified**, not a claimed product failure or passing test. The [committed-outcome follow-through](#committed-tool-outcome-proof) below supersedes this slice's normal rejected/aborted-call audit gap; full Code Mode/MCP call-origin and hostile/held recovery workloads remain unqualified. Seatbelt/S4, model budgets, product grant/standing-rule management, cross-device/offline answers, high-risk drafts/owner proofs, actual cloud calls, the iOS engine, HMAC anchors, lock/iCloud and release/device/kill gates remain open. Production tools stay off; S1 results count only on an accepted `slice-N`.

<a id="committed-tool-outcome-proof"></a>
#### Committed tool-outcome follow-through (2026-10-26)

- **Scope:** the next bounded D90/P0-06 development slice on unchanged candidate `892585fc8d8be3093b58c34aacffcd4e68c3a8d9`. No Pi working-tree change, new tool-enabling path, signed-UI rerun or S1 acceptance is implied.
- **Committed facts:** [`PolicyToolAudit.swift`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/PolicyToolAudit.swift) observes actual SDK terminal records and immutable result entries. Pi can reject an unoffered call inside `pi.generation` without creating `pi.tool`; that call uses its genuine result-entry receipt, not an invented task. A rejection that skipped policy hooks gets an explicit denial, not a fictitious hook invocation or allow.
- **Preparation is not completion:** mandatory before/dispatch enforcement and both-form scrubbing remain. `afterTool` records only redaction accounting/sticky exposure before Pi commits. A supported synchronous commit subscriber captures content-free final nested metadata before task-scoped SDK documents retire; durable `tool.completed` follows the actual committed error/success and precedes the application task's finish.
- **Durable identity:** [`Store+PolicyToolAudit.swift`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Store/Store+PolicyToolAudit.swift) binds fenced conversation + actual runtime receipt to immutable original app-task/call/name/parent attribution. Completion, the plain A mirror and its replay receipt commit together. Indexed assistant-entry attribution does not follow the latest `activeRequest`. Unique pre-migration legacy facts stay original; this is not retroactive SDK-source proof or an event/HMAC backfill.
- **Regression proof:** the unavailable-call regression first completed a model turn with zero decision/completion facts. The integrated **13 Store + 6 runtime tests** now pass: pre-hook rejection, throwing authorizer privacy, real abort/normal close of parent + approval-pending child, post-hook nested schema failure, stale fences, receipt/identity conflicts, migration cutoff, old-turn attribution and reopen with a reused provider call ID. Pi's primitive coercion is allowed; invalid-input coverage omits a required field instead of pinning coercion behavior.
- **Actual standalone smoke:** outside the test runner, the real pinned Pi/runtime/SQLite produced **five** matching event/mirror completion facts across four controlled external-HTTP scenarios. No completion/file preceded Allow once; the approved note was actually written; invalid/unavailable attempts did not write; nested schema failure was recorded as failure; normal reopen preserved the original facts. A separate normal no-tools runtime using real `local/qwen3:0.6b` answered **8** to **3 + 5**. [Observed result](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-tool-outcomes/runtime-smoke.json).
- **Integrated checks:** complete [`scripts/ci.sh` output](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-tool-outcomes/final-checks.txt) passed over **210 Swift files**, **195 tests across six packages**, 31 accepted dependency occurrences, schema drift, Mac/universal iOS Simulator builds and bundle/notices checks. No wire shape or new event type was added.
- **Qualification boundary:** normal cancellation and reopen are proved; forced SDK-event overflow, held kill/recovery and all Code Mode/MCP origins are **not qualified**. If a post-crash nested outcome lacks both an application receipt and recoverable SDK metadata, reconciliation fails closed; it never infers success from the parent. Production tools and the broader gates above remain disabled/open. S4's confinement/design scorecard, product grants/standing rules, outcome checks and tagged conformance are next, not completed.
- **Cleanup/preservation:** the controlled provider stopped normally with exit 0; temporary provider/CLI source, executable, grant and fixture state were removed. The existing signed Mac review app/home, prototype data and Pi sibling were untouched. [Scope and qualification record](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-tool-outcomes/verification.json).

<a id="s4-confinement-kernel-gate"></a>
### S4 confinement kernel-gate proof (2026-10-26)

- **Result: failed early gate, no winning design.** Outside-repository native Swift prototypes compared srt's filesystem rule ordering with an independent deny-default Seatbelt profile. These are filesystem/kernel experiments, not a complete Swift srt port, egress gateway or shipped `Sandbox` module. D77's any-escape rule blocks promotion of both.
- **Actual surface:** `/usr/bin/sandbox-exec` on this arm64 developer Mac, observed macOS **27.0.1 / 26A434**. Fresh synthetic homes/grants/canaries and owned loopback/Unix listeners only; no personal SSH files, Keychain contents, clipboard contents or external network target. This is not reference-hardware performance acceptance.

| Check | srt-ordered filesystem prototype | Own deny-default profile |
|---|---|---|
| Raw native boundary probes | 31/34 pass; 3 hard-link failures | 31/34 pass; 3 hard-link failures |
| Real PiEnv file/exec probes | 13/16 pass; 3 hard-link failures | 13/16 pass; 3 hard-link failures |
| Six intended-allow raw controls | All pass | All pass |
| Confined daemon, normal EOF | Exit 0 | Exit 0 |

- **Controls and failure:** the separate unsandboxed SDK control passed all 16 cases. Both confined profiles allowed reading and modifying an outside synthetic inode through a preexisting hard-link name inside a grant. Renaming the alias retained raw read access; real `RemoteExecutionEnv` file operations and `exec` reproduced it. New outside hard-link creation, symlink access, direct outside/other-home access, configured `.env` reads, read-only writes, protected config/hook writes, owned loopback/Unix connections and the tested privileged-binary/Mach lookups were denied. Those narrow passes do not cancel the escape.
- **Rejected false pass:** the initial own-profile run denied all six legitimate controls because Foundation's URL normalization emitted `/var` while fixtures used `/private/var`. That run was not safety evidence. Both final generators use POSIX `realpath`, and the final matrices require functioning positive controls.
- **Real SDK integration:** unchanged candidate `892585fc8d8be3093b58c34aacffcd4e68c3a8d9`; public `EnvDaemonSession`, `ProcessEnvTransport`, `EnvConnection` and `RemoteExecutionEnv`, not a mock. A temporary native entrypoint received an ephemeral PSK through its owned stdin pipe, never argv/environment/files. Explicit daemon environment and `inheritEnv=false` exec passed synthetic inheritance checks. All three SDK sessions ended normally with status 0; the EOF-only adapter avoided intentional process-group kills. This is not held kill/child-teardown qualification.
- **D77/D81 guard prerequisite:** the pinned SDK's `Sources/PiEnv/Protocol/Daemon.swift` fixes `private let files = DaemonFiles()` and exposes only `init(transport:)`. Ordinary file writes follow links and mutate in place; no supported descriptor-guarded file-service injection exists. A host-side string check or transport precheck cannot make those later pathname operations race-free. The sibling and checkout were not patched.
- **Reference correction:** the actual srt `v0.0.81` tag is `c40cf14a65d025e3cd27f071f13a73c12b3b1f92`; inspected HEAD is `98a4016d27609ed34dd1fb43d23ed6efb6eeeb77`. Both already emit late nested/glob read denies after read allows; the old blanket secret-precedence concern was inaccurate. The [immutable README explicitly leaves macOS preexisting hard links unpromised](https://github.com/anthropics/sandbox-runtime/blob/98a4016d27609ed34dd1fb43d23ed6efb6eeeb77/README.md#L1119).
- **Next prerequisite:** qualify D77's separate unprivileged macOS bot account plus Seatbelt, after human authorization/provisioning. It is not assumed to cure the alias problem. No account, permissions or security configuration was changed. A Linux VM remains later in D77's fixed order and only for Linux work; no fallback is enabled before its full suite passes.
- **Still open:** complete external-sandbox PiEnv conformance (the upstream main fixture is hard-wired to local/in-process environments), the daemon guard and atomic writes, full path-class/race fuzzing, per-bot gateway lists and credential masks, LAN/private-range checks, toolchains, attributed violation logs, TCC prompts and reference-hardware cold-start/p95 thresholds. Closed-network probes do not qualify the gateway. S4/P0-12, tool-enabling contracts and Phase 0 remain incomplete.
- **Evidence and preservation:** [raw own-profile results](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-s4-confinement/strict-kernel-results.json), [raw srt-ordered results](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-s4-confinement/srt-port-kernel-results.json), [real SDK results](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-s4-confinement/sdk-kernel-results.json) and [scope/replay/hashes](../../evidence/2026-10-26-memoshi-s4-confinement/verification.json). Source and result copies match original SHA-256; the reference licence is retained. Owned temporary executables, sources and fixtures were removed; listeners closed and daemons exited normally. The existing signed review app/home, prototype data and Pi sibling were untouched. No production code or protocol changed, and the earlier 195-test CI record was not rerun or counted as S4 proof.

<a id="s4-separate-user-setup"></a>
#### Separate-user fallback: early read gate failed (2026-10-27)

- **Authorized scope:** the user selected **Set up isolated test account** for D77 and reports completing creation, initial login and the setup guide. Credentials stayed in native macOS UI. No automatic account/group, system-policy or personal-folder permission changes, remote-login/sharing service or broader device/kill gate is authorized. Test preparation changes only newly owned synthetic fixtures and their report drop.
- **Observed identity:** `id -u memoshibot` returned **502**, distinct from the main session's **501**. Its groups are `staff everyone localaccounts _lpoperator com.apple.sharepoint.group.1 com.apple.sharepoint.group.2`; no `admin` membership. Initial GUI login is user-reported, not an assistant-observed UI test.
- **Measured run, not setup error:** the actual UID-502 run completed. Its regular-file report was owned by UID 502 at mode `0644`; real/effective UIDs were both 502 and the admin GID was absent. The host-owned executable remained byte-identical to preparation. [Exact report](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-separate-user/report-a0a626d7-c049-441a-8dea-ab5dc573f38e.json), [verdict/provenance](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-separate-user/verification.json), [preparation/hashes](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-separate-user/preparation.json) and [native source](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-separate-user/separate-user-probe.swift).

| Actual UID-502 session | Checks passed | Normal daemon EOF exit |
|---|---|---|
| Unix-permission control, no Seatbelt | 18/18 | 0 |
| Separate user plus Seatbelt | 15/18 | 0 |

- **Failure and controls:** host-owned mode-`0600` originals and aliases were unreadable even without Seatbelt. For the mode-`0644` host-owned synthetic `.env`, the direct outside pathname was denied by Seatbelt, but its same-inode name inside the read-only grant remained readable through raw `open/read`, `RemoteExecutionEnv.readTextFile` and SDK `/bin/cat` exec. Granted reads, private-home write/append/truncate/rename/read, shell-marker detection and `inheritEnv=false` controls passed. The account protects owner-private data; that does not meet the stricter accepted inode-alias/folder contract.
- **Verdict and limits:** D77's any-escape rule rejects this preliminary separate-user candidate too. This was an early **read-only** link gate; it does not establish an outside-write escape under UID 502 or claim complete environment conformance, gateway/masks, toolchains, TCC, attributed violations or reference-hardware performance. The pinned SDK still has no supported descriptor-guarded file-service injection seam. Production tools, S4/P0-12 and release remain blocked.
- **Cleanup/preservation:** `/Users/Shared/memoshi-s4-cross-user-9b_wdq6f` and its synthetic fixtures/report drop/executable were removed after checksum-matched retention. Both daemon exits were observed normally; the driver requested private UID-502 scratch cleanup, which was not independently inspected through that user's private directory. The `memoshibot` account is left intact. No privileged service, password handling, account/group change or real-folder permission change was performed by the assistant. The signed review app/data, unrelated prototype and Pi sibling were untouched.
- **Setup history:** the completed temporary account guide was already removed. Its [retained source](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-account-setup/setup.sh) and [historical checks](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-account-setup/verification.json), including the unchanged unused template-helper diagnostic, remain separate from the cleanly compiled/linted native test launcher.
- **Subsequent explicit decision:** the user selected **Private Mac working copies**, now [D96](plan/decisions.md#d96), rather than a Linux-only workflow. The [new qualification proof below](#s4-private-working-copies) supersedes the earlier proposal-only status, not the failed measurements. No scorecard threshold is relaxed. If the native route fails, D77's remaining Linux VM is still unprovisioned/unqualified and only applies to Linux-compatible work; live host-folder sharing is not a qualified replacement.

<a id="s4-private-working-copies"></a>
#### Private working copies: filesystem/policy development proof (2026-10-27)

- **Accepted scope:** D96 qualification under the existing main UID **501**, using only newly owned synthetic source folders, private copies/stores and an owned in-process loopback provider. No new account, live-folder permission widening, privileged helper/service, personal data, credential, paid/cloud model or Pi_Swift edit. `memoshibot` remains intact; no further account-switch step is needed for this route.
- **What changed:** trusted no-follow descriptor admission imports independent file data; the sandboxed PiEnv daemon gets no live source root. Edits remain private. An immutable proposal is exported by trusted host file-data code only after mandatory runtime policy, an applicable durable approval and fresh authority/conflict checks. This is complete qualification source retained outside the production repository, not a shipping tool implementation.

| Actual native qualification | Observed result |
|---|---|
| Admission | **4/4**: ordinary bytes copied to a different inode; symlink, multiply-linked regular file and FIFO refused before daemon launch |
| Private-copy isolation | **23/23** raw/PiEnv/source-effect checks: legitimate edits work; direct live/outside reads and writes fail; new outside hard links and late symlink read/write/export are refused; copied `.env`/uppercase variants denied while `.env.example` reads work |
| Durable policy/export | **8/8** scenarios through real RuntimeAdapter, mandatory PolicyHooks, PiDurable and SQLite/replay, using controlled local SSE replies; one Allow once applies the exact create/replace proposal, all seven negative cases never invoke the export executor |

- **Policy observations:** originals stayed unchanged while approval was pending. Deny, removed/replaced synthetic authority, a newer outside source edit, changed staged bytes, and altered digest/preview arguments blocked export. A previously allowed card did not override fresh authority/conflict failure. Actual committed tool outcomes preceded `task.finished`, agreed with replay and survived normal store/runtime close and SQLite reopen in every case. The driver supplied approval responses; this is not a rendered/signed approval-UI or product Grant-record qualification.
- **Evidence:** [exact native report](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-working-copies/working-copy-results-c67e4ff6-0b72-441d-a1b5-edd3b5c0c91a.json), [verification/scope/hashes](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-working-copies/verification.json), [actual command output](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-working-copies/native-output.txt), [compile recipe](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-working-copies/compile-command.json), [descriptor-copy actor](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-working-copies/WorkingCopy.swift), [raw/SDK driver](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-working-copies/WorkingCopyMain.swift) and [real policy driver](../../evidence/2026-10-27-memoshi-s4-working-copies/PolicyExportSmoke.swift). The retained report is **190,115 bytes**, SHA-256 `395fb5452d1b74aa6033afacb943f139433e00263cceab61016a0e419613e69e`; all retained source/report bytes match their originals. Native compilation with warnings-as-errors and the final CLI both exited **0**.
- **Limits and next gate:** full S4/P0-12 remains open. The pinned SDK lacks supported in-daemon descriptor-guard injection and the complete conformance suite through an external daemon. Production grants/bookmarks/read-only/revocation and original-source sensitive-path mapping/export, high-risk owner verification, full Code Mode/MCP/call-origin/hostile fixtures, gateway/masks, toolchains, TCC, attributed violations and reference-hardware performance are not accepted. The content-only actor explicitly refuses directory-set and intentional mode/group changes; delete/repeat/partial-effect and held recovery qualification are not claimed. Per-file atomic replacement is not a multi-file transaction or non-cooperating external-writer inode/content CAS.
- **Normal shutdown and preservation:** daemon EOF exit **0**, all private working-copy removals and provider shutdown were observed. After checksum-matched retention, the owned source/executable/module-cache/fixture root was removed; corrected `/bin/test ! -e` independently passed (the first cleanup post-check used the nonexistent `/usr/bin/test`). No deliberate kill/device case ran. The signed review app/homes, unrelated prototype, actual Application Support, Pi sibling and test account were untouched. Production tool set remains empty; Code Mode, MCP, Apple connectors and the iOS engine stay disabled. D90's accepted-slice/release hold and D87's calendar remain unchanged.

<a id="source-grant-development-proof"></a>
#### Local folder grants and original-source export proof (2026-11-01)

- **Implemented backend:** [`FolderGrantRecords`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Store/FolderGrantRecords.swift), [`Store+FolderGrants`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Store/Store+FolderGrants.swift) and schema 6 persist local-device/bot ownership, read/write access, terminal revocation/expiry, revisions, physical root identity and private bookmark bytes. Lifecycle changes and their typed `grant.granted/revoked/expired` audited facts commit together; access changes advance the revision. Revocation, downgrade and observed expiry cancel dependent pending approvals/cards atomically. Allowed decisions remain historical, never current authority.
- **Original-source enforcement:** [`SourceFolderAuthority`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/SourceFolderAuthority.swift) creates/rebinds real Foundation bookmarks against strict no-follow physical-root traversal and device/inode identity. Every runtime `fs.read/fs.write` definition must supply trusted source targets. [`PolicyHooks`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/PolicyHooks.swift) rechecks current Store authority and filesystem/bookmark identity at authorization and dispatch, and binds exact grant revisions, original paths, classes and target identity into the immutable memo. A private staging path cannot substitute for its source or take the bot-home policy cell. Read-only grants cannot yield write approvals in any mode; file reads mark output private.
- **Conservative path scope:** [`SourcePathPolicy`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/SourcePathPolicy.swift) implements original-namespace project/secret/host-exec/git-internal/instruction/CI/system/Memoshi-state classification and grant-root restrictions. Unsafe spellings are refused; case/NFC/default-ignorable comparisons and protected subtrees are conservative and can over-classify. Trusted callers must supply every protected Memoshi/Pi/engine state root and configured external hooks root. This is not full filesystem/race conformance. Raw secret access, host-exec patches, instruction changes and direct git-internal writes remain denied where their dedicated safe workflow is absent; CI writes follow §4.7's attended sensitive-path friction.

| Real native grant-backed export | Observed result |
|---|---|
| Allow once, CI in Auto and explicitly confirmed Bypass | Exact private proposal exported only in the three permitted cases; Auto's CI change required approval |
| Deny, read-only grant, revoke, downgrade, staged/source conflict, changed digest | No export; revoke/downgrade canceled the real pending approval/card |
| `.env`, `AGENTS.md`, `.git/config`, `.git/objects/controlled-object` in Bypass | Original protected class retained; no export |
| All 14 scenarios | No tool export before any required decision; committed result matches the real effect; audit agrees with replay; grant/audit survive normal SQLite reopen; private copy removed |

- **Evidence:** [actual report](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-source-grants/source-grant-results.json), [scope/checks/hashes](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-source-grants/verification.json), [native output](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-source-grants/native-output.txt), [compile command](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-source-grants/compile-command.json), [native driver](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-source-grants/SourceGrantSmoke.swift), [unchanged descriptor-copy actor](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-source-grants/WorkingCopy.swift), [failing-before access regression](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-source-grants/regression-red.txt) and [exact check output](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-source-grants/checks-output.txt). The report is **68,299 bytes**, SHA-256 `18a99c69ca1970144fe9b8305256250d90be5617361383e8dc22d3b5c69986ee`. The owned loopback provider received 14 discovery/28 completion requests, no other request; no real inference/cloud call or credential was used. This driver did not start PiEnv or repeat the earlier kernel-isolation proof.
- **Checks and qualification limit:** all six packages passed **238 tests**; final Core rerun passed Store 58 + Runtime 57; format/boundaries covered 221 Swift files. The nine standalone non-toolchain checks passed, including schema, licences, Mac/universal iOS Simulator builds and bundle layout. **`scripts/ci.sh` did not pass:** this Mac has Xcode 27.2 / Swift 6.4.2, versus pinned 27.1 / 6.4. No pin or checker was weakened; restore the pinned toolchain or explicitly decide its upgrade before pinned-toolchain qualification.
- **Open at this proof's cutoff:** grant picker/review and authenticated management RPCs, device replication/merge, standing rules, high-risk owner verification, safe secret/patch/instruction workflows and full source/copy/export integration. The [subsequent folder-management proof](#folder-management-development-proof) below completes the local picker/RPC and toolchain follow-through, not the other gates. Expiry is observed on relevant access/answer, not by a new background grant sweeper. The reused content-only copy actor remains external qualification code, not a shipping tool, multi-file transaction or non-cooperating external-writer CAS. The supported SDK daemon-guard/external-conformance seam, full origins/hostile/recovery cases, gateway/masks, toolchains, TCC and reference-hardware performance still gate S4/P0-12. D90's accepted-slice/release hold remains. Product tools, Code Mode, MCP, Apple connectors and the iOS engine stay disabled; Pi_Swift, the signed review copy/data, prototype and `memoshibot` are unchanged.
- **Normal shutdown and cleanup:** the native process exited 0 after closing its owned provider, runtimes and stores; all 14 private copies were removed. Actual retained source/report hashes matched before deleting the owned scratch root `/private/tmp/memoshi-source-authority-zhhm84qh`; `/bin/test ! -e` then passed. Retained evidence is outside the production repository. No account switch, privileged process, signing change, real-device action or deliberate kill ran.

<a id="folder-management-development-proof"></a>
#### Native folder management and stable-toolchain qualification (2026-11-01)

- **Toolchain decision and result:** [D97](plan/decisions.md#d97) adopts installed Xcode **27.2 / Swift 6.4.2**, retaining XcodeGen 2.46.0 and exact baseline checks. New stable versions are qualified and recorded, not automatically downloaded or accepted as floating `latest`. Canonical **`scripts/ci.sh` passed**: 244 tests across seven test products, format/boundaries across 229 Swift files, tokens/licences/schema, XcodeGen, Mac/universal iPhone/iPad Simulator builds and bundle layout. [Exact stdout](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/ci-stdout.txt) and [stderr](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/ci-stderr.txt) supersede the preceding proof's historical mismatch; no deployment target, Swift language mode, Pi/dependency pin or release hold changed. Since 2026-11-19 the baseline is XcodeGen 2.47.0, qualified by D97's procedure in canonical-ci-16 ([work of 2026-11-19](#work-2026-11-19)).
- **Implemented management:** [`FolderGrantMethods`](../Packages/MemoshiProtocol/Sources/MemoshiProtocol/Methods/FolderGrantMethods.swift), [`RPCSession+FolderGrants`](../Packages/MemoshiDaemon/Sources/MemoshiDaemon/RPCSession+FolderGrants.swift), [`RuntimeAdapter+FolderGrants`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/RuntimeAdapter+FolderGrants.swift) and the existing schema-6 Store expose negotiated `bots.grants.folder.v1` to the real signed app role only. Local `bots.grants.list/update/revoke` derive user authority in the daemon. New grants bind the picker's device/inode identity to the helper's own physical capture; existing changes use the original path/current revision. Exact broad-path confirmation, protected-root refusals, terminal states, atomic pending-card cancellation/audited facts and durable original receipts remain authoritative. Historical retry cannot restore newer restricted/revoked access. [Current bounded wire contract](specs/protocol.md#current-folder-management).
- **Native surface:** [`FolderGrantSetup`](../apps/mac/Memoshi/FolderGrantSetup.swift) and [`FolderGrantView`](../apps/mac/Memoshi/FolderGrantView.swift) use the real Chat's Assistant identity, one event consumer and folder snapshots applied before bootstrap/replay acknowledgment. Choose Folder → native Review Folder → explicit grant; access/revoke have explicit review. Loading, unsupported, unavailable and error states are distinct. Uncertain metadata requests retain the exact original ID for explicit Retry, never automatic mutation retries or optimistic receipt state.

| Real native scenario | Observed result |
|---|---|
| System picker and review before Grant | Correct physical path, Read only selected; authenticated snapshot still contained no new grant |
| Grant, then explicitly change access | Active read revision 1, then active write revision 2 |
| Normal Done → Quit → reopen | Same active write record, IDs and revision; new helper PID and no pending mutation |
| Move the owned source, refresh, narrow, revoke | Unavailable guidance; restriction to read revision 3 and terminal revocation revision 4 both succeeded |
| Restore the source and normally reopen again | Exact revoked record persisted; no access/revoke controls and no resurrected authority |
| Existing no-folder helper with a separate fresh home | Explicit Folder Management Unavailable, no fabricated empty list, Choose Folder disabled |

- **Real IPC/durability:** the separately Apple Development-signed app-role driver used production same-FD authentication and the negotiated capability. Selection-identity mismatch, changed-key reuse, stale revisions and `/` were refused. Original create/access/revoke requests replayed after a real helper restart without restoring authority; a genuine signed peer claiming CLI was refused. Closed-store SELECTs observed six update/two revoke receipts and matching six grant/two revoke durable and audit facts, all with `actor.kind: user`; no provider or policy tool call was configured. [Actual RPC flow](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/rpc-exercise.json), [reopen replay](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/rpc-replay-after-reopen.json) and [closed-store observations](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/closed-store-observations.json).
- **Evidence and visual check:** [verification/scope/hashes](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/verification.json), [actual native steps](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/native-steps.json), [automation limits](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/native-automation-notes.json), [read-only appearance](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/native-readonly.png), [reopened revoked appearance](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/native-reopened-revoked.png), [actual compile/sign recipe](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/compile-and-sign.json) and the retained native drivers/captures sit outside both source repositories. Both appearances were inspected. Keyboard injection did not open Go to Folder; only public AX selection/button actions and Done-before-Quit are qualified, not keyboard shortcuts or quit with an open sheet.
- **Scope at this proof's cutoff:** local folder metadata for the local Chat bot, capped at 64 active grants and a list of all active plus recent ended records up to 200. No standing rules, grant replication/merge, existing-expiry editing or background expiry sweeper. Best-effort root availability is not full race/TCC/volume/hung-mount/performance qualification. At this cutoff the descriptor-copy/export actor was external; the [following runtime-library proof](#runtime-working-copy-development-proof) integrates it without closing SDK/full-S4/product-composition gates. The immutable Pi candidate and D90 **Leave release blocked** remain unchanged. Tools, Code Mode, MCP, Apple connectors and the iOS engine remain disabled; P0-20 lock/iCloud and S5 release/device gates are not closed.
- **Normal shutdown and cleanup:** all owned native app launches and the separately owned old helper exited 0 normally; all eight app/helper PIDs were absent. Both synthetic source fixtures retained SHA-256 `7dcbffb34cb0a2cf50e401486554f767213bd172696ac9f8bfe3878a8a581e81`. The 32 retained source/capture/output/recipe artifacts matched their actual hashes before removal; [cleanup receipts](../../evidence/2026-11-01-memoshi-folder-management/cleanup.json) record removal of only the owned scratch, two new isolated homes and native fixture. An initial picker listing of unrelated Documents filenames was excluded and removed. Existing signed review app/data, prototype, Pi_Swift, `memoshibot`, profiles and repository signing defaults were preserved. No credential, model inference, cloud/paid call, account switch, security-setting change, physical-device or deliberate-kill run occurred.

<a id="runtime-working-copy-development-proof"></a>
#### Runtime-owned private-copy/export integration (2026-11-02)

- **Implemented interface:** [`RuntimeWorkingCopy`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/RuntimeWorkingCopy.swift) admits a current [`SourceFolderBinding`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/SourceFolderAuthority.swift) for its persisted owning Chat into protected private application state. [`PrivateWorkingCopy`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/PrivateWorkingCopy.swift), [`WorkingCopyFileSystem`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/WorkingCopyFileSystem.swift) and [`WorkingCopyTree`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/WorkingCopyTree.swift) now contain the real descriptor-owned mechanism, not an external proof-only actor. Opened source identity is checked before copying; source entries pass original-namespace policy before read. Unsafe links/special entries and protected source classes refuse the entire admission. Private file identities are independent. Admission-failure cleanup deletes only objects it created; unknown/moved-in objects cause a reported cleanup refusal.
- **Actual policy integration:** a static, non-replay-safe `exportTool` may be composed before edits/proposals exist, but runs only for an explicitly frozen exact proposal and current write authority. Registration and model discovery share the same Chat-filtered names. The mandatory existing hooks bind grant revisions, original source targets, policy version and encoded argument digest; the executor rechecks authority and source/stage state. UTF-8 with a BOM stays byte-exact, binary uses base64, and Unicode-equivalent but byte-different submitted text is refused. No new schema, wire method, capability, dependency or model-controlled export-authorizer switch was introduced.

| Actual production-library native scenario | Observed result |
|---|---|
| Allow once: ordinary, BOM-prefixed UTF-8 and binary replace/create/delete | Exact frozen bytes exported; no source effect while approval waited |
| Deny, read-only grant, revoke and downgrade | No batch effect; changed grants canceled the real pending approval/card |
| Changed source/stage or byte-different submitted arguments | Known batch refused, outside source edit preserved |
| Newly staged `.env` in Bypass | Original secret class denied; no export |
| Owned immutable-file failure after earlier batch effects | `added.txt` created and `note.txt` replaced; `remove.txt` remained. Committed `tool_failed`, explicit unknown-outcome model error and no fresh proposal; no all-or-nothing claim |
| Another real Chat given the export definition | Export capability absent from its model tools; owner source unchanged |

- **Checks:** the native executable linked the actual `MemoshiRuntime` archive, with **no legacy `WorkingCopy.swift` input**, and passed **12/12 export scenarios plus cross-Chat isolation**. Its owned HTTP fixture saw 13 model-discovery/25 completion requests and zero other requests; no model inference/cloud call/credential was used. Audit matched replay and grants/audit survived normal SQLite reopen. Eight new consumer test functions in two suites passed, including admission links/FIFO/protected inputs, source-root/Chat/private-container ownership, exact BOM/binary approval/export and immutable argument conflicts. Canonical **`scripts/ci.sh` passed 252 tests**, format/boundaries across 235 Swift files, tokens/licences/schema, XcodeGen, Mac/universal iPhone/iPad Simulator builds and bundle layout on D97's toolchain.
- **Evidence:** [actual report](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-runtime-working-copies/runtime-copy-results.json), [verification/limits/hashes](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-runtime-working-copies/verification.json), [native stdout](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-runtime-working-copies/native-stdout.txt), [actual compile command](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-runtime-working-copies/compile-command.json), [native driver](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-runtime-working-copies/RuntimeCopySmoke.swift), [canonical stdout](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-runtime-working-copies/ci-stdout.txt) and [stderr](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-runtime-working-copies/ci-stderr.txt) are outside both repositories. Report: **91,823 bytes**, SHA-256 `b822cfe8fa2481b00a2beca327b5bbd0af7ed2b989e8f778f2294bac4503cb40`. The initial missing-interface compile failure and corrected public-doc/two-closure lint failure are retained separately; neither is presented as a runtime failing-before reproduction.
- **Precise limits:** content-only data, not a general file editor. Directory/mode/ownership edits are refused; ACL/xattr/resource-fork fidelity and frozen-proposal size/time/tree ceilings are not qualified. Live copies do not resume after process restart: use fresh trusted binding/copy/proposal handling after inspection, not automatic export. No external-writer CAS or multi-file transaction. The source-reviewed scalar path-splitting correction closes a component-parsing gap [INFERENCE]; no hostile symlink-race reproduction or full race/cleanup conformance is claimed. A same-UID mkdir/identity-observation race remains. The native executable did not run PiEnv, repeat kernel isolation or verify UI/TCC/reference-hardware behavior.
- **Remaining gate and preservation:** the immutable SDK candidate still needs supported in-daemon guarded-file-service injection/external daemon conformance. Product PiEnv/tool composition, metadata/recovery/resource bounds, gateway/masks, full hostile/origin/effect/recovery and reference-device gates remain open. D90 **Leave release blocked**, the accepted-slice requirement, D87 dates and device/intentional-kill hold are unchanged. Normal daemon tools stay `[]`; Code Mode, MCP, Apple connectors and iOS engine remain disabled. Pi_Swift/pin, prototype data, existing signed review apps/homes, `memoshibot`, profiles and signing defaults were preserved.
- **Normal cleanup:** the native executable exited 0 after joining its owned provider and closing runtimes/stores. All 13 private copies were removed by normal close. Only the synthetic `remove.txt` flag was set for the partial-effect case and explicitly cleared before teardown. Fourteen retained artifacts matched actual hashes before [owned scratch cleanup](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-runtime-working-copies/cleanup.json); `/private/tmp/memoshi-runtime-copy-7ptbqxoi` was removed and absence checked. The initially overlarge raw report read failed hash verification; paged raw reads then matched the complete original before retention. No account/security-configuration, service/signing/profile change, physical-device or deliberate-kill action occurred.

<a id="working-copy-metadata-development-proof"></a>
#### Content-only metadata refusal and renewed native proof (2026-11-02)

- **Problem and implemented boundary:** a real user-attribute admission regression first failed because the copy succeeded without its metadata. The [runtime metadata profile](specs/policy.md) now rejects unsupported metadata on proven descriptors before copying/exporting, rather than normalizing or stripping it. `RuntimeWorkingCopyError.unsupportedMetadata` distinguishes admission/proposal refusal. OS-managed provenance is the sole name exception; its bytes are never read or copied. Full metadata-preserving editing remains open.
- **Native metadata proof:** seven actual admission refusals preserved user attributes, FinderInfo, a resource fork, directory attributes, file/directory ACLs and a nonzero file flag. Two private file/directory metadata edits refused proposals, preserved their metadata until close and did not reach the original. Four additional real approval-pending file/directory metadata cases refused the whole export with unchanged originals and unchanged introduced metadata. These used isolated synthetic data, not personal files or a changed account/security configuration.
- **Renewed runtime proof:** the executable linked the actual production `MemoshiRuntime` archive, without a legacy copy-engine source. All **16 export scenarios plus cross-Chat isolation** passed, including ordinary/BOM/binary exports, Deny/read-only/revocation/downgrade, frozen content/argument conflicts, protected paths and partial/unknown effects. The old immutable-file fixture is now correctly inadmissible: the renewed partial-effect case instead uses an owned readable/nonwritable nested directory. Earlier writes succeeded, its final deletion failed, committed/model-visible outcome was unknown, and a fresh proposal was refused. The owned directory was restored for cleanup; no immutable-flag bypass was added.
- **Checks:** **10 consumer test functions in two suites** passed; full canonical `scripts/ci.sh` passed **254 tests across seven test products in six packages**, format/boundaries over **237 Swift files**, schema/licence/tokens, XcodeGen, Mac/universal iPhone/iPad Simulator builds and bundle layout on Xcode 27.2 / Swift 6.4.2. The native fixture saw **17 model-discovery/33 completion requests, zero other requests**; the external HTTP provider was controlled, with no real inference, cloud call or credential. Real approvals, committed outcomes, replay and normal SQLite reopen remained in the path. This was a native executable smoke, not a repeated UI, PiEnv, TCC, physical-device or kill/recovery run.
- **Evidence:** [actual report](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-metadata/runtime-copy-results.json), [verification/limits/hashes](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-metadata/verification.json), [native stdout](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-metadata/native-stdout.txt), [compile recipe](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-metadata/compile-command.json), [canonical combined output](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-metadata/ci-output.txt) and [cleanup receipt](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-metadata/cleanup.json) stay outside both repositories. Report: **136,576 bytes**, SHA-256 `9f339212430f05c96dbeb9e1aeb4176e53e8cf96e3953721b09eee49c832e055`.
- **Limits and preservation:** this closes silent ACL/attribute/fork/flag loss for the conservative profile, not general metadata fidelity or full S4. Resource ceilings, copy-session recovery, full race/CAS/cleanup qualification and the supported SDK daemon-guard/external-conformance seam remain open. All 19 returned private copies closed normally; seven refused admissions left empty private containers. The native process/provider exited normally and only the owned proof scratch was removed after retention. D90 **Leave release blocked**, the Pi candidate, device/kill hold and disabled product tools remain unchanged. Pi_Swift, actual prototype/review data, signed review apps, test account, profiles and signing defaults were untouched.

<a id="working-copy-limits-development-proof"></a>
#### Working-copy resource ceilings and bounded cleanup (2026-11-02)

- **Implemented:** [`WorkingCopyLimits`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/WorkingCopyLimits.swift) adds trusted, tightening-only development ceilings behind the existing copy interface. Admission/all rescans bound single-file and aggregate bytes, global descendant entries and component depth; capture bounds changed-file count and total frozen bytes before retention. Canonical arguments include escaping/base64/paths/hashes, and successful-export ceilings bound retained replay/inode history. Untrusted submitted arguments are compared against the frozen shape and exact UTF-8 before this module performs serialization; existing policy/audit hashing remains. The [policy spec](specs/policy.md) owns the precise guarantees and limits.
- **Native proof:** **20 resource cases** exercised exact and one-over file/tree/global-entry/depth/raw-data/change-count/canonical-JSON boundaries, escaping growth, invalid widening and canceled admission. Two intentionally overgrown private shapes refused a proposal and truthfully returned/remembered `cleanupRequired`; their leftovers were only removed with the outer owned synthetic fixture, not by a product recovery fallback. **20 actual export scenarios** retained real Pi/policy/SQLite/approval/replay/reopen behavior, adding source/stage growth, an unchanged **108 KiB unreviewed text** refusal, a byte-exact **24 KiB binary approved export**, and exhausted successful-export budgets. Nine metadata refusals and foreign-Chat isolation also passed: **50 native checks** total.
- **Checks:** **15 consumer test functions in two suites** passed, including an actual failing-before oversized-admission regression. Final canonical `scripts/ci.sh` passed **259 tests across seven test products in six packages**, **238 Swift files**, format/boundaries/schema/licences/tokens, XcodeGen, Mac/universal iPhone/iPad Simulator builds and bundle layout on Xcode 27.2 / Swift 6.4.2. The controlled provider saw **21 discovery/41 completion requests, zero other requests**. There was no real inference, cloud request or credential; the executable linked the actual runtime archive with no legacy copy-engine source.
- **Fixture diagnosis:** the first oversized-argument stress pass denied promptly, but the scripted model's 8K context triggered Pi compaction. The driver answered that summary request as a final continuation and left the real generation waiting. Read-only SDK/application traces and retained Pi/Store facts identified it; the smoke now advertises 262,144 context and explicitly requires the real tool-continuation result. The original oversized input is unchanged and now passes refusal/completion checks. Production model settings, Pi compaction/retry behavior and the SDK were not changed. The actual approval-event 64 KiB cap informed conservative argument headroom; existing target/redaction/event limits still apply.
- **Evidence:** [actual final report](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-limits/runtime-limits-results.json), [verification/limits/hashes](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-limits/verification.json), [native output](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-limits/native-stdout.txt), [compile recipe](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-limits/compile-command.json), [canonical combined output](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-limits/ci-output.txt) and [cleanup receipt](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-working-copy-limits/cleanup.json) stay outside both repositories. - **Limits and preservation:** bounded admission/scans/capture are not kernel-enforced live-write quotas, hard wall-clock/interruptible-filesystem guarantees, global concurrent-copy/disk budgets, full hostile cleanup/race/CAS qualification or reference-device performance. Restart/outcome recovery remains separate; a fresh binding/copy/proposal is still required, never automatic export. No PiEnv/product-tool bridge, protocol/schema/capability/dependency, signing/profile/account change, physical device or deliberate kill was introduced. Normal processes/providers closed, retained hashes matched, and only the owned scratch (including explicitly reported synthetic cleanup leftovers) was removed. Pi_Swift/pin, signed review apps/data and prototype data were untouched. D90 **Leave release blocked**, the accepted-slice gate and disabled tools remain.

<a id="working-copy-ownership-development-proof"></a>

These direct Store/lifetime fixtures are not a Pi checkpoint-gap or process-kill proof.

The final executable saw **23 model-discovery/45 completion requests, zero other requests** from the controlled provider; no real inference, cloud request or credential was used.
