<a id="consumer-follow-through-proof"></a>
#### Consumer follow-through and qualification evidence (2026-11-03)

This is D90 development on unchanged candidate `892585fc8d8be3093b58c34aacffcd4e68c3a8d9`, not a `slice-N`, SDK qualification or Phase 0 completion. Production tools remain empty. Physical-device and deliberate process/VM-kill gates, **Leave release blocked** and **Keep broader qualification blocked** are unchanged.

- **Persistent local recovery boundary:** schema 8, [runtime recovery/admission](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/RuntimeAdapter+Threads.swift), [durable queue](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Store/Store+ChatQueue.swift) and [protocol implementation scope](specs/protocol.md#current-implementation-scope) add a storage-only whole-conversation hold, pre-effect unsafe intents and an exact original-request outbox bounded to 32 inputs/262,144 encoded UTF-8 bytes. A thrown callback or invalid nested result cannot release uncertainty. Queue cancellation is distinct from stopping a claimed turn or settling an interrupted step. The [policy record](specs/policy.md) distinguishes this from missing SDK waits, outcome checks and Run again/Skip/Mark as done.
- **Race proof, not just compilation:** real Store/Pi regressions reproduced canceled-release drain conflicts, a newer send overtaking an older held input, and a receipt for input that was neither queued nor submitted. [Failing-before](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/recovery-races-before.txt), [initial passing-after](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/recovery-races-after-initial-fixes.txt) and the [exact bounded stress extension](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/recovery-race-stress-source.swift) retain those sequences; the two temporary stress tests were removed after the 48/96-attempt post-fix run. A further [concurrent-send red](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/recovery-fifo-before.txt) reproduced Store/FIFO admission bypass. The [final five Store/five Runtime functions](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/recovery-fifo-after.txt) pass with strict original-input precedence and one joined held-open activation. An unavailable older queued input blocks newer sends until admitted or explicitly canceled; no model fallback or reordering is hidden. Permanent tests cover semantic transitions, bounds and precedence, not source text or defaults.
- **Signed replay and sequence-zero rebuild:** [37/37 checks](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/replay/replay-report.json) passed through real daemon/client/transport/store code: 512 initial commits, 1,000 reconnect cycles (990 disconnects and ten real helper EOF/restarts), 190 commit/connect races and a final normal restart. Live and fresh sequence-zero clients each applied/acknowledged 2,702 facts with zero gaps/duplicates; a fresh Store fold matched their exact chain. Schema 8 opened 8→8, retention stayed 42 days and device identity stayed stable. Twelve server lifetimes and the verifier exited 0 after owner EOF; eleven token rotations were observed. Helper FDs stayed 14 at steady checkpoints and returned to 3; client stayed 9→9. The final stop is fact 2,703, beyond the compared prefix. This is Memoshi's feed, not SDK replication or deliberate-kill evidence; the [source/signing manifest](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/replay/PROOF-MANIFEST.txt) fixes its snapshot. Its harness was never committed. Since 2026-11-19 the current replay record is the rerun on schema 15 with an in-repo harness, 17 of 17 ([work of 2026-11-19](#work-2026-11-19)); this run stays the record of its source.
- **Synthetic environment conformance:** the [initial run](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/environment/original-report.json) passed 28 safe cases each for local and unsandboxed remote environments; sandboxed remote passed 26 and exposed two failures from the missing `/private/var/select/sh` read. A data-only literal allowance [still failed](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/environment/data-only-selector-report.json). The exact Memoshi-owned `(allow file-read* (literal "/private/var/select/sh"))` correction [passed the two affected cases](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/environment/selector-read-report.json), with outside-canary reads still denied. It was a targeted rerun, not a new complete 28-case run; the signal-kill case stayed excluded. The SDK guarded-file seam, hard-link escape findings and all remaining S4 thresholds are not waived.
- **Code Mode inputs and SDK handoff:** [H1–H4](research/spikes/2026-11-02-pi-swift-consumer-handoff.md) and the [versioned workload record](research/spikes/2026-11-02-code-mode-workload.md) were supplied through the single authorized SDK request pointer. W1/W2 Debug diagnostics and CLI failure/cleanup checks are not Release/two-backend/device/p95 qualification. The limits were proposed **at filing**, then accepted by [D98](plan/decisions.md#d98); the later SDK notice reports input/contract acceptance and development delivery. The [2026-11-06 reconciliation](research/spikes/2026-11-02-pi-swift-consumer-handoff.md#current-delivery-reconciliation-2026-11-06) records the missing accessible answer/source and qualifying-baseline conflict. No SDK source, pin, cache or hold changed.
- **Native ChatGPT subscription host:** [app-owned facade](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/ChatGPTSubscription.swift), typed `cg_` references and native Model Settings use real PiAI OAuth, a separate per-reference SDK Keychain service in the app-only access group, device-only persistence and at-most-60-second broker access-token leases. The helper never receives the refresh token. No Pi/Codex/session/environment credential is imported, and neither cloud nor local inference is an automatic fallback. The earlier synthetic callback/PKCE/cancel/refresh smoke used real SDK callbacks with memory credentials; it was not real Keychain/cloud sign-in.
- **Normal-Quit refresh ordering:** the real pinned PiAI refresh diagnostic [failed before](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/subscription/drain-before.txt) because caller cancellation let `close()` return before rotated credentials were saved. The narrowly shielded SDK resolution [passed all eight checks after](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/subscription/drain-after.txt): rotation persisted before close, canceled caller got no lease, normal leases remained bounded and each scenario refreshed once. This uses synthetic HTTP and memory storage, not an OpenAI account or OS Keychain. Import boundaries stayed unchanged; the reproducer is outside the repo.
- **Native review isolation:** the review copy uses its own CFBundleIdentifier/preferences domain while preserving the exact expected sealed signing IDs, existing Apple Development identity/profile, app-only secret group and empty helper entitlements. A throwaway LaunchServices probe proved that `CFFIXED_USER_HOME` isolates Foundation runtime paths but **not** `UserDefaults`; existing Memoshi preference domains were therefore not reused. The private-copy namespace/profile distinction is development evidence, not release identity/notarization qualification.
- **Native gallery:** the [original avatars and component comparisons](specs/design-reference.md#6-original-avatargallery-and-subscription-ready-development) were inspected in the signed Mac app, including Current/Warm, appearance/accessibility comparisons, shape/color/size inventory and status/control/offline transitions. [Captures and AX records](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/native/ui/run-b/) remain private Memoshi evidence. The real layer-backed diagnostic found and fixed one-shots exceeding the shared animation cap; it does not measure P-IDLE/P-STREAM/P-AMBIENT/P-HIDDEN.
- **Native browser boundaries/lifetime:** two explicitly created profiles kept their own probe cookies across an actual normal Quit/relaunch and saw none of the other's. The [typed final action report](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/native/ui/run-c/browser-action-report.json) records fifteen actions: normal native Type/Click and script Select/Click/scroll, an opaque-origin frame click, five credential refusals and two submit-control refusals. Every refused action has no observed input and no navigation. Three native actions on two synthetic page hosts delivered 59/59 trusted target key/mouse events; this is not the missing three-page/hidden-host or real-site suite. Takeover disabled lab actions; Hand Back restored review. Closing every window and reopening through the actual menu-bar extra now reattaches the same selected view/document without reload, rather than leaving it parked. Script text via WebKit `execCommand` can produce trusted events; the label no longer incorrectly promises all script events are untrusted.
- **Native cold-start failure fixed:** before any WebKit store/view was initialized, static identifier enumeration and later owned-profile removal each crashed at `0x48` in WebKit's callback `RunLoop::dispatch`. Unneeded global enumeration was removed. Required removal now initializes and releases **that exact owned store** before the static deletion call, with no other identifier/store enumeration, dummy view or relaxed trust. The [owned-namespace probe](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/native/WebKitRemovalProbe.swift) and [actual signed-app cold removal](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/native/ui/run-d/cold-removal-result.json) are separate evidence; the latter confirmed only the created profile, removed its store/list entry and left the app running. Its ambiguous AX confirmation reply is not the success authority: the actual post-state and native completion log are. The proof-write volume-full incident was separate from the symbolized WebKit crash; only the generated diagnostic module cache was reclaimed, not SDK caches or review data.
- **Pre-authorization checks and ready app:** [canonical checks before the disclosure fix](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/ci-before-subscription-disclosure-fix.txt) passed **276 tests**, format/import boundaries across **283 Swift files**, tokens/schema/licences, generated projects, Mac/universal iPhone+iPad Simulator builds and bundle layout on Xcode 27.2 / Swift 6.4.2. The [review signing record](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/native/ui/run-d/signing.json) and [native ready screen](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/native/ui/run-d/model-settings-ready.json) record the original `/private/tmp/memoshi-native-review/run-20261103-d/Memoshi.app` and isolated `home`, before account authorization. The user subsequently completed native sign-in; the [real-cloud proof below](#real-cloud-chat-proof) owns the later signed update, unchanged account/data and actual model observations.

The [ten-minute configured closed-window run](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/native/ui/run-b/browser-closed-window-report.json) is deliberately **not** an S3.5 pass:

| Closed-window measurement | Observed | Unchanged gate / limitation |
|---|---:|---|
| Detached samples with every app window closed | 115 | All sampled points had no host window |
| DOM and image snapshots succeeded | 115/115 each | Finite samples, not continuous observation |
| Longest sampled continuous span | 597 s | 600 s required; with 5 s sampling from 5.3 s to 602.0 s this is probably a sampling artifact, not a stall [INFERENCE] |
| Minimum sampled timer rate | 0.918 Hz | ≥1 Hz required |
| Median sampled timer rate | 0.968 Hz | Also below 1 Hz; 80 of 115 samples were under 1 Hz ([recomputed values](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-s3-s4-draft-reports/derived-measurements.json); the run's own report rounds to 0.9 and 1.0 Hz) |

Reference-Mac timings, thirty-page snapshots, hidden-host native input, the twenty-task/five-site suite, gateway/packet capture, sign-in Card fill/canaries, per-profile WebContent memory, complete VoiceOver/keyboard review and held mobile/device rows remain unqualified. Manual review capabilities do not enable bot tools or waive any S3/S4 requirement.

Evidence is retained outside both repositories under [`2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification`](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/), without regenerated compiler caches or diagnostic binaries. The following real-account record supersedes the earlier cloud prerequisite. Lock/iCloud behavior, SDK wait/fence/guard integration, product tools and held device/release gates still require their own proof.

<a id="real-cloud-chat-proof"></a>
#### Real ChatGPT cloud, Stop and normal-reopen proof (2026-11-03)

The user completed **Sign in with ChatGPT** in the native app. Actual inference used **`openai/gpt-6.1-sol` through PiAI and PiDurable**, not a local model, mock transport, API-key substitution or imported Pi/Codex/session credential. The app's own data-protection Keychain held the account; no token, credential database or runtime database is included in retained proof.

- **Real replies:** the first cloud turn computed `37 × 31` as **1147**. After normal app Quit/reopen, a fresh turn computed `46 × 29` as **1334**, without repeating sign-in. [First turn](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/cloud/first-turn.json) and [post-reopen turn](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/cloud/post-reopen-real-turn.json) retain only synthetic test prompts/results and native message identities.
- **Actual streaming Stop:** the native Stop button was enabled while a nonempty cloud partial was visible. One press returned the UI to Send in approximately **0.4 seconds**, preserving the partial under the same message ID. The durable turn is **`canceled`**. The [Stop observation](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/cloud/stream-stop.json) is a single functional observation, not S1 latency/performance qualification. An earlier long turn completed while the document window was closed; the app process stayed alive and that request was not resent.
- **Normal restart, exact history and account reuse:** the updated signed app reused the existing `review.20261103d` preference namespace and `/private/tmp/memoshi-native-review/run-20261103-d/home`; neither credentials nor databases were copied. All three pre-reopen turn identities/statuses (`done`, `done`, `canceled`) and six message IDs/Pi entry references matched afterward. The stopped partial's exact displayed text survived, and the canceled generation did not resume. A new successful cloud turn after reopening proves the saved account remained usable. This was normal Quit/reopen with terminal tasks, not a held kill/active-run crash or replicated-device recovery experiment.
- **Disclosure corrected:** actual subscription mode initially said “OpenAI · API usage” and warned of API-key charges. [`LiveChatComposer`](../apps/mac/Memoshi/LiveChatComposer.swift) now uses the existing configured credential kind to show **ChatGPT subscription** and account usage limits; API-key mode retains its separate API-usage notice. [Before](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/cloud/subscription-disclosure-before.json), [after/reopen](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/cloud/native-reopen-observation.json) and the [actual native surface](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/cloud/real-cloud-stop-and-reopen.png) record that correction. No source-text/wording-pinning test was added.
- **Final verification:** [canonical CI after the disclosure change](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/cloud/ci-disclosure-fix.txt) passed **276 tests** and checks across **283 Swift files**, with Mac/universal iPhone+iPad Simulator builds and bundle/licence/schema/token checks. The running updated app is `/private/tmp/memoshi-native-review/run-20261103-e/Memoshi.app`, using the existing `d/home` above. It preserves production sealed signing IDs, the existing authorized Apple Development profile and empty helper entitlements; its private preferences-domain variant is not release identity/notarization qualification.

[Consolidated real-account evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-02-memoshi-consumer-qualification/cloud/real-cloud-proof.json) distinguishes exercised behavior from limits. The Pi candidate remains `892585fc8d8be3093b58c34aacffcd4e68c3a8d9`, not `slice-N`. **Leave release blocked**, **Keep broader qualification blocked**, physical-device/deliberate-kill holds and disabled product tools/Apple connectors/iOS engine are unchanged. Real refresh-token rotation, locked/unattended access, iCloud sync and the remaining S1–S6/Phase 0 gates are not supplied by this smoke.

<a id="phase-0-follow-through-2026-11-03"></a>
#### Debug menu, chaos harness and IPC follow-through (2026-11-03)

D90 development on the unchanged candidate, after the user's [2026-11-03 decisions](#8-next-moves). Deliberate kills ran only on the Mac, against processes this work started, in isolated synthetic homes with controlled loopback models. None of it is a `slice-N`, gate or device result.

- **P0-09 debug surfaces:** the Development menu adds **Kill memoshid** (confirmed; SIGKILL of the supervisor's own authenticated app-owned helper PID only, while Quit and stop stay non-killing), **Drop Connection**, **Inject Latency** (Off/100/500/2000 ms for this session, through the client's [development link conditioner](../Packages/MemoshiClient/Sources/MemoshiClient/DevelopmentLinkConditioner.swift), which keeps frame order and peer checks) and **Show HUD**. A read-only **Tasks** window lists turn tasks and their children; with no `tasks.list` RPC, it derives IDs from loaded history, the live turn and approval cards, then calls `tasks.get`. The [supervisor](../apps/mac/Memoshi/DaemonSupervisor.swift) now restarts its app-owned helper after an exit it did not request (after 0.5 s, then 2 s) and stops after 3 unexpected exits within 5 minutes, shown as **Daemon stopped**; no LaunchAgent is registered.
- **Native smoke** (signed review copy, new isolated home and preferences domain, loopback model because Ollama was not running):

| Step | Observed |
|---|---|
| HUD on open | Protocol 1, schema `10ca4fc54232`, connected, app-owned PID, applied seq with a contiguity check |
| Kill memoshid | Helper 21159 replaced by 23765 in about 1 s; seq 15 → 16, contiguous, no resync |
| 500 ms injected latency | First delta 4,391 ms and turn 5,500 ms, against 584 ms and 3,237 ms without |
| Drop Connection, 500 ms still on | Reconnected in about 5.25 s; seq 22 → 24, contiguous |
| Tasks window | 3 turn tasks, all done, owned by the Assistant Chat |
| Normal Quit | App and helper exited without SIGKILL |

  The first run exposed a defect, now fixed: a restarted helper refused the next send because its model list stayed empty until rediscovery; the app now rediscovers models when a new helper instance answers. memoshid overhead stays **unavailable** in the HUD because the protocol carries no daemon-side provider timings. Not exercised: an approval surviving a kill (production tools are off), the crash-loop stop, 100 and 2,000 ms in the app, nested tasks, VoiceOver and keyboard review. [Evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-p09-debug/).

  A code review after the smoke found no blocker; its fixes are covered by package tests, not a rerun of the signed smoke. A restart now disconnects the client before probing, so a dead connection's cached hello cannot abort it; quit cancels in-flight work before waiting for a parked restart; the Tasks window ignores cancellation and reloads for IDs that change mid-load. With latency **Off** the conditioner returns the authenticated carrier unchanged, keeping transport backpressure; turning a delay on or off reconnects once and replays from the applied cursor; held frames are bounded by 10,000 frames and 8 MiB, and overflow fails the link.
- **IPC fallback socket fixed:** the smoke also showed that every data root too long for `run/memoshid.sock` shared `/tmp/memoshi-<uid>/memoshid.sock`, so two app copies collided: the second helper exited 70, and its client reached the other copy's helper and was refused at hello. [`IPCPaths`](../Packages/MemoshiIPC/Sources/MemoshiTransport/IPCPaths.swift) now names the fallback `memoshid-<first 16 hex digits of the SHA-256 of the canonical root>.sock`, with a regression test. A helper still running under the old name is not found by a new client until it exits.
- **P0-15 harness, runtime-host level:** the non-shipping `MemoshiQualification` library and `memoshi-qualification` tool in `Packages/MemoshiDaemon` ([README](../Packages/MemoshiDaemon/Sources/MemoshiQualification/README.md)) provide the effect-ledger server (its own process, append-only JSONL, idempotency keys from the new `RuntimeToolInvocation.effectKey`), canary generation with an encoding-aware scanner, and a seeded chaos driver. The driver SIGKILLs a host child that runs the real Store, RuntimeAdapter and PiDurable against a scripted loopback provider. The signed memoshid/client protocol level and a real-model proxy are follow-ups.

| Smoke batch | Result |
|---|---|
| 10 runs, each kill point twice | 8/10. Resubmissions returned original receipts; approvals reattached to the same card; effect-posted kills deduplicated (2 attempts, 1 effect); resume to ready p50 30 ms, p95 57 ms. Both streaming kills failed |
| 5 runs with memoshid's startup order | 4/5; the streaming run ended `failed (model_failed)` |
| 4 kills during one pending approval | Pass: reattached each time, then completed |
| 5 kills during one pending approval | Fail: allowed after reattaching, but the tool never ran and the turn stayed running |

  Every run had 0 duplicated effects, 0 effects before a decision and 0 canary hits. Defects found: (1) after a mid-stream kill, the visible transcript keeps the aborted partial as the turn's reply although Pi committed the final answer (reply binding in `Store.projectChatTurn`); (2) memoshid resumes before model discovery, so a resumed generation finds no model (`no_model`); (3) five kills during one wait reach Pi's restart limit of 5, the known H1/E6 SDK limit. Defects 1 and 2 are fixed [below](#runtime-follow-through-2026-11-03); 3 waits for the SDK. [Evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-p0-15-harness/).
- **Records:** [S3](research/spikes/2026-11-03-s3-webkit-browser-draft.md) and [S4](research/spikes/2026-11-03-s4-this-mac-sandbox-draft.md) draft scorecards; the [provenance and licence review](research/2026-11-03-provenance-and-licence-review.md), with [phase-0 §7](plan/phase-0.md#port-list) complete; [D98](plan/decisions.md#d98) and [D99](plan/decisions.md#d99).
- **Checks:** after the review fixes, [canonical `scripts/ci.sh`](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave1-ci/ci.txt) passed on Xcode 27.2 / Swift 6.4.2: the new prerequisite step, **302 tests**, format and module boundaries across **305 Swift files**, tokens, licences (31 occurrences), schema drift, Mac and universal iPhone/iPad Simulator builds and bundle layout. The review's harness fixes also made a clean 4-run batch (approval-waiting, tool-executing, effect-posted and idle kills) exit 0 with full cleanup. Code landed as `577b3fa` (IPC), `7f6fa23` (P0-09) and `0a37a99` (P0-15).

<a id="runtime-follow-through-2026-11-03"></a>
#### Recovery fixes, providers, tool origins and S3 follow-through (2026-11-03)

D90 development on the unchanged candidate; four slices ran in parallel and a code review found no blocker. Its findings (one major, four minor) were fixed before commit. Kill runs used only processes this work started, isolated synthetic data and controlled or local models.

- **Recovery fixes (P0-15 defects 1 and 2):** a turn's reply is now bound once, when its submission settles, from the run's committed Pi entries: Pi's answer, or for a failed or stopped turn the last partial with text, so a failed retry never replaces the partial the user saw (failed replies carry stop reason `error`). `RuntimeAdapter.start()` no longer contacts providers, so the socket listens at once; `resume()` looks up unfinished turns' models after the listener is up, and a thread whose model stays unknown (a keyed provider before the app reconnects) stays paused (`RuntimeStatus.awaitingModels`) until `modelsList()`. Crash-image tests failed before each fix. Harness: scripted 10/10 (streaming kills 2/2, resume p95 29 ms) and memoshid-order 5/5 after the review fixes. A new real-model mode routes through a canary-checking loopback proxy; local `qwen3:0.6b` passed 7/10, and the three failures were the model skipping the tool twice and returning an empty answer once [INFERENCE: from the stored records], with 0 duplicates and 0 canary hits. Pressing Stop while a thread waits for its model is untested. [Evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-recovery-fixes/).
- **Providers and usage (MOD-01, S1.3, E11):** one [provider table](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/ModelProviderCatalog.swift) drives validation, Store naming, PiAI construction, discovery, the credential lease and the Mac picker. Anthropic (`x-api-key`) and OpenRouter (Bearer, OpenAI-compatible models only) join OpenAI and Local, proven only against loopback stand-ins; no real key or cloud call was used. Each key is accepted only in its declared header, once, at its provider's origin, and keyed requests use a [transport that refuses every redirect](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/ProviderTransport.swift) (a 307 test showed PiAI's default transport carried `x-api-key` to the second host). Turn usage now covers every request in a run; cost is in µUSD and known only for per-token, catalog-priced requests. One local `qwen3:0.6b` turn matched Ollama's own usage exactly (1 of S1.3's 20 comparisons). [`scripts/check-signin-flows.sh`](../scripts/check-signin-flows.sh) runs in CI after the app builds and failed on both planted fixtures. [Evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-providers-usage/).
- **Tool origins (S1.8–S1.10):** an explicit `RuntimeExtensionComposition`, empty by default, adds Code Mode (WasmKit, `models: false`, no globals, 268,435,456 bytes, D98's 300 s ceiling), `tool_search` and a stdio MCP bridge (`inheritEnv: false`, reviewed tools only, deferred `mcp__<server>__<tool>`, secrets registered with the scrubber). Extension tools need an explicit policy entry and a `Wrap.tool` dispatch recheck; `policy.decided` carries `via` (`model`, `code_mode`, `mcp`). A 200-call run: every call decided and audited, the 194 that ran rechecked at dispatch, 100 nested calls attributed to their script, 6 blocks (4 refusals, 2 throwing hooks) with no execution, 4 token-bearing results scrubbed in both forms; Code Mode's discovery globals ran without a decision, as E21 predicts. A SIGKILL at step 3 of a 5-call script resumed without rerunning steps 1–2. The ceiling is wall-clock from the script's first start: a nested approval still pending then is canceled with the script, and a host returning after it ends the script without further calls (both tested in Debug WasmKit). memoshid and the app still offer no tools. Not run: remote HTTP MCP with OAuth, Keychain OAuth storage, Ask-first approvals for nested or MCP calls, unsafe nested steps. [Evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-tool-origins/).
- **S3 follow-through:** the closed-window shortfall is WebKit's 1 s alignment of a hidden page's repeating timers, whatever `inactiveSchedulingPolicy` says; the override is private API. Bot-browser runs now keep the page in a visible one-pixel host window: 11 minutes with every app window closed gave 659 samples, rate min 8.755, p50 9.952 Hz for a 10 Hz timer, 658 s continuous and 659/659 snapshots. A detached page stays at exactly 1 Hz (marginal). A development sign-in Card filled a canary login into the offline lab page only while origin, commit and both fields still matched; the page received both values, filled fields became masked credential fields, and scans of the run's files and 186.6 MB of unified log found 0 hits. Per-profile WebContent footprint: 16–20 MB parked, 36–38 MB live, 68–69 MB in the visible host. A sleeping or locked display is expected to throttle the host window again [INFERENCE]. [Draft scorecard](research/spikes/2026-11-03-s3-webkit-browser-draft.md), [evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-s3-browser/).
- **Checks:** after the review fixes, [canonical `scripts/ci.sh`](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave2-ci/ci-after-review.txt) passed: **344 tests**, format and module boundaries across **323 Swift files**, licences (31 occurrences), schema drift, Mac and universal iPhone/iPad Simulator builds, the new sign-in-flow check and bundle layout.

<a id="multi-conversation-device-proof"></a>
#### Conversations, real child tasks, mobile engine and device prototype (2026-11-03)

Development on the same read-only Pi candidate, not a `slice-N` or Phase 0 verdict. Normal app/helper composition still has no tools, MCP, Code Mode, environment or subagents.

- **Conversation/runtime cutover:** persistent bot profiles, each bot's Chat and independent lead-bot Sessions have separate Pi stores. Model/persona/job defaults apply to the next request; local reasoning effort is visibly unsupported rather than silently accepted. Schema 9 adds atomic content-free `bot.created`, `bot.updated`, `chat.created` and `session.created` facts; schema 10 adds immutable child ownership/checkpoints. Both daemon and in-process routes use the same sanitized known-error mapping. [Implemented protocol subset](specs/protocol.md), [emitters and scope](specs/events.md).
- **Real children, not synthetic task rows:** trusted qualification composition can register actual foreground/background Pi subagents with bounded inherited capabilities and original-parent attribution. `tasks.graph`, `tasks.history` and targeted `tasks.abort` resolve real owned work; content-free child progress never inserts child transcript into a root turn. Foreground/background checkpoint SIGKILL regressions preserve one real effect. They do **not** cover the SDK nested-result-retirement window: a terminal `pi.tool` may retain only `kind: nested` after its parent-scoped result document is retired, before the app has persisted its receipt. Recovery fails closed; [H1/E20 acceptance remains open](research/spikes/2026-11-02-pi-swift-consumer-handoff.md).
- **Actual Mac surface:** a genuinely signed temporary app/helper exercised Assistant + three bot Chats + a Session, create/edit profiles and model-wire changes, isolated drafts/history, helper-kill recovery and real Stop. A separate trusted fixture seeded a real completed Pi child while the normal app was stopped; the normal no-tools helper then served its graph/history in Tasks. Native inspection exposed missing selectable-text accessibility and an overwritten Stop identifier; both were fixed, rebuilt and visually rechecked. [Final child surface](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave3-integration/native/mac-final-child-surface.png), [owned kill observation](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave3-integration/native/mac-final-kill-observation.json). The terminal child's Stop was disabled; active targeted cancellation is exercised through real typed-engine tests, not claimed as a native UI click.
- **Signed text-protocol kill harness:** a separately signed current `MemoshiClient` and normal helper used same-FD peer authentication, hello, Unix WebSocket and `RPCSession`, not private pipes. All five conversations delivered focused deltas and authoritative live text before one owned SIGKILL. Automatic reconnect authenticated the replacement helper; all five original send intents returned the exact original message/task/write-sequence receipt, all five isolated histories settled once, and original root graphs ended done. The observer loaded actual bootstrap snapshots and applied 41 post-baseline facts through head/cursor 44. Client and final owner-EOF helper exited 0; killed life exited signal 9. [Result](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave3-integration/native/signed-protocol-final/result.json), [reproducible Swift client](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave3-integration/native/SignedConversationSmoke.swift), [owned-process driver](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave3-integration/native/SignedConversationDriver.py). Earlier fixture owner-pipe/oracle failures are retained, not passes. This text-only proof does not qualify signed approval/card routing, nested retirement, the full chaos matrix or SMAppService.
- **iOS in-process engine:** the final owned iPhone Simulator UI run passed streaming, Stop/partial retention, relaunch/history, approval Deny/Allow once and Home/return. Its actual effect file contained only the one allowed note, no denied effect. Final xcresult (the bundle is not in the evidence folder and was not found on this Mac; noted 2026-11-13), [summary](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave3-integration/native/ios-final-summary.json), [screens](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave3-integration/native/ios-final-attachments/). An earlier run failed with a full disk and did not reach Deny; its failure is retained. Only owned scratch was removed. All observed continued-task submissions were refused with code 1; actual OS-granted continuation/expiration and physical-device behavior remain unqualified.
- **S1 runtime measurements:** the non-shipping `s1` CLI passed its invariants with one five-stream owned SIGKILL, five replayed receipts, five isolated Pi stores and 17 unique turns. Provider endpoint attempts numbered 22, including five interrupted requests, not 22 settled answers. These measurements use actual codec/typed requests over private pipes; they are **not** the signed IPC timings above.

| S1 host measurement | Samples | p50 | p95 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| First settled-conversation open/resume/history in a fresh host | 30 | 4.824208 ms | 8.380333 ms |
| Eight concurrent send admissions | 8 | 19.039792 ms | 20.874084 ms |
| Real retention-setting commit with eight streams open | 64 | 0.144375 ms | 0.475083 ms |
| Host physical footprint, ten open / four streaming | 20 | 14,221,984 B | 14,221,984 B |
| Host physical footprint, zero open | 3 | 6,554,248 B | 6,570,632 B |

The [raw report and boundaries](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave3-integration/s1/s1-measurements.json) retain CSVs, full samples and event envelopes. Open/admission/commit clocks exclude pipe transit; retention measures the app Store transaction, not Pi's internal commit duration. Host footprint excludes provider/driver and is distinct from resident size or lifetime max RSS. This Debug Mac16,6 has 38,654,705,664 bytes of RAM; it is neither reference Mac. Idle closure/deletion were unmeasured at that revision; the later [S1 lifecycle proof](research/spikes/2026-11-04-s1-runtime-draft.md) supersedes that gap, not the outstanding reference budgets.

- **Device prototype at the 2026-11-03 revision (historical):** trusted host-only exact identity enrollment, confirmed transitions, authoritative membership/lease/digest checks, reserve-before-commit and successor catch-up guarded direct and relay paths. An authority outage refused incoming work without destroying the encrypted stream; reconnect revalidated authority and caught up. Reachable authority remained required; missing plaintext or unresolved unpublished reservations blocked takeover. That revision's in-memory state was not MLS/cloud/Pi/mobile/deployed capacity. Later native MLS/durable-state and competing-write controls are recorded in the [current S2 draft](research/spikes/2026-11-04-s2-devices-draft.md); the [original commands and load limits](../Packages/MemoshiDaemon/Sources/MemoshiQualification/README.md) retain their scope.
- **Current S2 full process proof:** repaired `s2 --load` exited 0 in 412.08 seconds: 50 fresh confirmed pairing probes, 100 handovers, 20 frozen-runner takeovers/stale-write refusals, 400 unique forwards with 40 original-ID repeats, and 12,642 accepted Chat/Session writes. No audited two-writer epoch, backwards epoch or accepted stale local write; relay canary hits 0 against 20,584 positive-control hits. Expiry-window commit count was 0, so this is not nonvacuous concurrent-writing proof inside that window. An earlier stream-loss failure was repaired without allowing offline authorization; the next run correctly rejected pairing probes missing from the trusted roster, which was fixed in the harness.

| Full S2 load profile, three workers | Simulated users | Conversations | Steady RPC attempts/s | Pooled renewal p95 (ms) | Scheduler lateness p99 (ms) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Active only | 30 | 240 | 239.990 | 0.442500 | 3.067012 |
| Idle periodically renewed | 30 | 1,200 | 341.021 | 0.833917 | 3.356458 |
| No periodic idle renewal | 30 | 1,200 | 250.979 | 0.444500 | 3.118978 |
| Scaled idle periodically renewed | 300 | 12,000 | 3,409.782 | 0.722125 | 6.135917 |

The [full raw report](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave3-integration/s2-current-full/s2-measurements.json) retains every worker's report and four integer-nanosecond CSVs. The [independent verifier](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave3-integration/verify_s2_samples.py) checked all 12 workers / 48 CSVs / 72 distributions and first/last windows, then matched attempted RPC counts to independent relay totals. Rates above use the entire cohort's earliest steady start to latest drained end, not nominal duration or averaged percentiles; latency/lateness percentiles pool actual samples. Two busy claim retries after asynchronous release are included; all workers had zero errors, failed initial claims/moves, expiry, fencing or recovery claims. Zero periodic idle renewal still allows finite 30-second wake leases; the client renewal-set count is not live authority lease count. Scenario CSVs are rounded milliseconds; only load CSVs retain exact nanoseconds. These synthetic Debug loads do not establish deployed/CloudKit capacity or close O10. The current run omitted `--real-expiry`; the older 301-second QR result remains historical.

- **S4's 2026-11-03 interim denial (historical):** the rejected `localhost:port` candidate admitted an owned same-port LAN listener; its immediate correction denied even the sandbox's own proxy. The later [exact Unix route](research/spikes/2026-11-03-s4-this-mac-sandbox-draft.md#4c-2026-11-04-exact-unix-route-and-verified-https) restores legitimate authenticated traffic without that TCP allowance. Neither revision qualifies H3/full D96 or enables production tools.
- **Canonical checks:** [final `scripts/ci.sh`](../../evidence/2026-11-03-memoshi-wave3-integration/ci-final.txt) passed in 183.26 seconds: **450 tests**, format/module boundaries across **416 Swift files**, 165 protocol fixture cases, licences (31 resolved occurrences), schema drift, Mac and universal iPhone/iPad Simulator builds, sign-in flows and bundle layout. Initial runs exposed missing public API documentation and historical-schema fixtures retaining the new child table; both were fixed. An incidental exact global event-list/cseq pin was removed rather than re-pinned; the real duplicate-projection identity/head/history assertions remain. Raw initial failures are retained alongside the final result.
