Three shards: the first run (1,040 s) ran its two Core shards one after the other on a shared SwiftPM scratch path ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-19-memoshi-s1-17-sharded/README.md#correction-2026-11-19)); with shard 2 on an APFS clone, the second run took 285 s under load 30–65, Memoshi's shards passing and Pi_Swift's failing only on its 5 known issues ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-19-memoshi-s1-17-sharded-2/README.md)). The bar looks reachable sharded; a quiet-machine or reference-box run is still the measurement.
- **S3 no-bypass control.** Not a pass. The head run failed F3, F4, F7 and F9. The same day F3 proved a product defect in `BrowserTaskRunner` and F4, F7 and F9 harness faults, all fixed in `045305c`; after that every direct and edge check passes. The gateway separate-profile scenario cannot start without an app-side seam, so 06 row 9 has no gateway result ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-19-memoshi-s3-no-bypass/README.md), [S3 §1a](research/spikes/S3-webkit-browser.md#1a-how-the-s36-answer-changed-after-the-draft)).
- **The `willSubmitForm` probe.** WebKit on macOS 27.0.1 never called `webView(_:willSubmitForm:submissionHandler:)` (either Swift form; `button.click()` or `requestSubmit()`; an `about:` or an `https` action), though `responds(to:)` is true, and every form POST reached the navigation policy. So every form decision comes from the navigation policy, and the `willSubmitForm` handler is a second check that does not run. The row 9 text of `BrowserReviewReport.swift` is corrected (`e0c47b5`). It is a candidate Apple Feedback, to file with the D135 reports ([probe](../../evidence/2026-11-19-memoshi-wkform-probe/README.md)).
- **S4-T7.** The first run timed only the first confined command and started no PiEnv daemon, so it does not measure row 7 ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-19-memoshi-s4-t7.README.md)). After that evening's test change the cold start includes the daemon's start: 1 bot p50 289.09 ms, p95 302.90 ms; 4 bots together p50 298.70 ms, p95 308.57 ms, against < 300 ms with no marginal band, so not a pass on this Mac (load 23–50). Exec of `/usr/bin/true` in one warm bot: p95 10.03 ms against a plain spawn's 2.93 ms, far inside the 50 ms bar ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-19-memoshi-s4-t7-daemon/README.md)). Four-bot exec and an unsandboxed `LocalExecutionEnv` baseline are still missing.
- **S4-T10.** The masked-credential test passed again, in a per-command Seatbelt instance, not inside the guarded daemon. `SandboxSocketProbeTests` shows `~/.docker/run/docker.sock` and a stand-in memoshid socket denied inside the sandbox; it kept no output, and no live Docker or memoshid socket was probed ([S4 §3](research/spikes/S4-this-mac-sandbox.md#3-outcome-table)).
- **Replay on schema 15.** 17 of 17 assertions with the new in-repo harness at `ad900b9`: 1,000 cycles, 10 helper restarts, 0 gaps and 0 duplicates; the live client and the seq-0 rebuild equal the receipts through head 1,996, and the store fold equals them through 1,997, the final stop ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-19-memoshi-replay-schema15/README.md)). It replaces the [2026-11-02 record](#consumer-follow-through-proof) (37/37 on schema 8, with a harness that was never committed) as the current one; UI-only frame focus and descriptor counts are not in the new harness.
- **Sandbox reaping.** Reaping detached `setsid` children by their launch environment was tried and dropped (`2b85f74`): macOS withholds the environment of platform binaries from `KERN_PROCARGS2`, and polling the process tree would miss a double fork, so a partial guarantee needs a decision ([remaining work §1.4](plan/remaining-work.md#14-engineering-owed-before-or-at-the-gate)).
- **Disk.** A full disk ended 5 kill runs; about 11 GB was free in the evening and 4.3 GB at 21:10, under the 15 GB clean-workspace floor.

**Review of the day's claims.** A read-only review checked the Markdown of `984756f..64c3191` and the 2026-11-19 evidence READMEs against the raw files and the code at `64c3191`, and found 21 problems: 3 P1, 9 P2 and 9 P3 ([claims review](../../evidence/2026-11-19-memoshi-claims-review/README.md)). The P1s: the first sharded run's Core shards did not overlap (C01); the serial S1.17 total was about 494 s, not 408 s, because "131 s" left out one of the two Pi_Swift test runs (C02); the first S4-T7 cold start started no daemon (C03). The rest: "marginal" where S4 has no marginal band (C04); a partial S4-T7 harness presented as complete (C05); the 11-07 outliers said not to recur in samples that do not cover them (C06); the masked-credential test said to run inside the daemon (C07); socket probes with no retained output (C08); the subagent batch marked done while the gate draft and the S1 report said it was owed (C09, C10); the 20-run batch's "invalid" runs and its date (C11); the third batch's failures called environmental without logs (C12); the sharded run's issue breakdown (C13); XcodeGen 2.47.0 recorded in only two places (C14); a docs item already done (C15); canonical-ci-16's commit and coverage (C16); stale gate-draft evidence links (C17); the replay chains stated as equal where they are equal as prefixes (C18); a lab-page claim with no run behind it (C19); this handoff out of date (C20); remaining-work's status date (C21).

**Fixed the same evening.**
- **Code and scripts** (`e0c47b5`): `scripts/test-contracts.sh` runs shard 2 from an APFS clone of the Core scratch, and a log with SwiftPM's waiting line makes the run invalid (exit 3); `scripts/run-replay-qualification.sh` honours `MEMOSHI_BUILD_ROOT`, builds with `--disable-automatic-resolution` and removes its work directory on any exit; `BrowserTaskRunner` writes each step's record before the cancellation check, so no step is left `inFlight`, and a cancel during the last task's profile discard also ends the run cancelled; the row 9 text of `BrowserReviewReport.swift`; `SandboxLatencyTests`' cold start includes the PiEnv daemon's start; the `apps/mac/project.yml` comment and `minimumXcodeGenVersion`. The S4-T7 rerun and the second sharded run ran on these changes before they were committed, on top of `64c3191`.
- **Documents:** every finding C01–C21, in the [README](../README.md), this handoff, the [remaining work](plan/remaining-work.md), the [gate draft](plan/gates/phase-0-review-draft.md), the [S1](research/spikes/S1-runtime.md), [S3](research/spikes/S3-webkit-browser.md) and [S4](research/spikes/S4-this-mac-sandbox.md) reports and the [qualification README](../Packages/MemoshiDaemon/Sources/MemoshiQualification/README.md), committed in `8e7bd5b`. The serial and first sharded S1.17, replay, S3, S4-T7, both 20-run subagent and canonical-ci-16 evidence READMEs end with a dated correction, and their original text is kept.

**Still owed from this day:** a full S2 run on the gate commit; a complete real-model `subagent` batch with published reports; S1.17 on a quiet machine or the reference box, and in CI; S4-T7's four-bot exec and unsandboxed baseline, and rows 6 and 7 on the reference box; S4-T10's retained probe output and live sockets; the app-side seam for S3's gateway scenario; the `willSubmitForm` Feedback ([remaining work §1.3](plan/remaining-work.md#13-runs-owed-that-need-no-owner-input)).

<a id="work-2026-11-20"></a>
### Fixes of 2026-11-20: Stop context and resumed replies

**Scope.** The two defects the [2026-11-19 Mac app test](../../evidence/2026-11-19-memoshi-app-ui-smoke/README.md#correction-2026-11-20) found. Development work; no gate row changes.

- **Stop, then a new question.** Pi leaves every aborted reply out of the model's context (`deriveContext`, then again `transformMessages`), in the same helper life and after a restart alike, so after a Stop the model saw two user messages in a row and could answer the older one. The app test's same-life request had carried a reply that finished before the Stop, not a stopped one. Now a `beforeRequest` hook ([`RuntimeAdapter+StoppedReplies.swift`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/RuntimeAdapter+StoppedReplies.swift)) puts each stopped reply back as a finished reply ending with a `[stopped]` note, and a turn none of whose reply reaches the model (stopped before its first delta, or whose stopped reply was calling a tool) gets an assistant message holding only the note ([protocol.md `chats.abort`](specs/protocol.md#current-text-chat-profile)). Tests: `RuntimeStoppedReplyContextTests`, with and without a runtime reopen.
- **A reply continued after a restart.** When a conversation first opens after a restart and Pi continues an admitted turn it had not settled, the Store records `task.resumed` with `by: system` ([events.md §5.5](specs/events.md#55-task)), and `chats.history` marks that reply and `live` with `resumedAfterInterruption`. The Mac and iPhone transcripts show "Interrupted · continued after a restart" under it; VoiceOver reads "Interrupted, then continued after a restart" ([`ResumedReply.swift`](../Packages/MemoshiApp/Sources/AppCore/ResumedReply.swift)). Tests: `RuntimeResumedTurnTests`, `ResumedReplyHistoryTests`, `InProcessChatModelResumedReplyTests`.
- **Live check** (signed Debug app, local `qwen3:8b` through a logging proxy): after a Stop during thinking and a kill of `memoshid`, the next request ended with the stopped essay request, the note-only assistant message and "Name two oceans, one line each.", and the model answered the oceans question. After a kill of `memoshid` mid-reply, Pi regenerated the turn (two model requests 3.5 s apart), the Store recorded `task.resumed {by: system}`, and the reply showed the note, also after Quit and relaunch ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-20-memoshi-stop-context/)).
- **iPhone Simulator** ([smoke run](../../evidence/2026-11-20-memoshi-ios-sim-smoke/README.md), iPhone 14 Pro simulator, iOS 27.0, Debug MemoshiMobile with its in-process engine, local `qwen3:8b` through the logging proxy; one ordered XCUITest, passed in 339 s): model setup, a reply, Stop mid-reply (settled at once, reply marked Stopped), the question after the Stop (the request carried the partial reply with the `[stopped]` note; the model answered the new question), approval Cards answered with Allow once and Deny, the app terminated mid-reply and relaunched (the engine regenerated the reply, no Resume choices, caption shown), persistence after a normal relaunch, the Python, Browser and Device Pairing Review sheets, the Others tab and landscape. Not covered: a physical device, VoiceOver itself, large text sizes, backgrounding.
- **Canonical CI.** [canonical-ci-18](../../evidence/2026-11-13-memoshi-canonical-s2/canonical-ci-18/README.md) passed on `983336e` with a clean tree: 17 of 17 steps in 886 s; 1,490 Swift Testing tests in 16 runs plus 6 XCTest tests, 105 release-tooling tests and the spike self-tests (66 + 2).

**Still open:** Stop in a subagent's conversation (only the main chat conversation is handled); a partial a crash cut off carries no note; no `task.resumed` with `by: user` after a Resume answer ([object-model.md, `resume` card](specs/object-model.md)); child tasks' resumes are not recorded; when Pi parks a run after 5 restarts, the Memoshi turn stays running. Pi_Swift report filed at [`2026-11-20-memoshi-stop-context/pi-swift-report.md`](../../evidence/2026-11-20-memoshi-stop-context/pi-swift-report.md): Pi drops an aborted reply twice, and uses `.aborted` both for a user's Stop and for a partial a crash left before a retry ([remaining work §1.4](plan/remaining-work.md#14-engineering-owed-before-or-at-the-gate)).

<a id="app-tests-2026-11-20"></a>
### App test round 2 of 2026-11-20: Mac, iPhone, iPad, Release, upgrade

**Scope.** Development checks on this Mac and the iOS Simulator, after `31f65ca`; no gate row changes. The one code change is `ba704ea`: a Debug-only `development_note` approval tool for the Mac app's own development helper (`-MemoshiDevelopmentApprovalTool YES` on the Debug app; shared with the iPhone as `DevelopmentApprovalProbe` in MemoshiRuntime; Release compiles none of it), without which no approval Card can appear on the Mac. [canonical-ci-19](../../evidence/2026-11-13-memoshi-canonical-s2/canonical-ci-19/README.md) passed on `ba704ea`: 17 of 17 steps in 855 s, 1,493 Swift Testing tests in 16 runs plus 6 XCTest tests.

| Area | Result | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Mac approval Cards | Card content, Allow once, Deny (the model is told), Waiting-for-You count, Approvals pane, a Card kept across a helper kill, VoiceOver values, Esc and ⌘Return: pass. Two defects below | [mac-round2](../../evidence/2026-11-20-memoshi-mac-round2/README.md) |
| Mac Browser Review | Offline lab and snapshot, lab click, sign-in canary, handoff, cookie isolation across relaunch, loopback fixture, the form gate (GET allowed; POST, Send, Buy and cross-site refused without Interact; one-shot confirmation), the attributed gateway (example.com through the proxy, non-granted sites refused), revoke, a 1-minute background run, profile removal: pass. Not run: Saved Test Login, recipes, uploads and downloads, Export | same |
| iPhone `python_run` from the Chat | Card (T2 · Execute), results (`338350`; numpy/pandas table), a failing cell, the 5 s timeout, Stop during a run (then Skip): pass | [ios-round2](../../evidence/2026-11-20-memoshi-ios-round2/README.md) |
| iPad (iPad Pro 11-inch, iOS 27.0) | The smoke steps in portrait; landscape except Python Review under load and one test-side lookup; layout; windowed sizes down to 375×486: pass | same |
| Accessibility audit (XCUITest) | 77 issues on iPhone at Large, 51 at XXXL, 137 on iPad: contrast, Dynamic Type, clipped and hidden text, one hit area, one label (`audit-summary.md`) | same |
| iOS Browser Review | Fixture, cookie stores across relaunch and reset, reload, snapshot, element selection, handoffs, recipe, offline sign-in document (Keychain unavailable in the Simulator), attributed HTTPS gateway: pass | same |
| Release builds | Mac (signed) and iOS Simulator build; no Debug-only name in either, also after `ba704ea`; layout check ok; iOS Release chat, Stop, relaunch pass and the development arguments enable nothing; the Mac Release app opens the development workspace, as every configuration does | [release-check](../../evidence/2026-11-20-memoshi-release-check/README.md) |
| Upgrade from older data | From `4658dcd` (schema 15, no migration) and from `f22517d` (schema 13: migrations 14 and 15 ran, `backups/pre-15.sqlite` identical to the old store, additions only, every old row kept): chats, bots, sessions, Stopped marks, Trash, folder grant, draft, Tasks all present; new turns and Stop work. The user's data was backed up and restored byte-identical | [mac-upgrade](../../evidence/2026-11-20-memoshi-mac-upgrade/README.md) |

**Defects and findings** (the first three fixed the same day, see [Fixes of 2026-11-20 (round 2 defects)](#fixes-2026-11-20-round2); the floating header, the accessibility issues, the SF Symbols, the Browser Review wording and the vnode messages were taken up on 2026-11-21, see [Work of 2026-11-21](#work-2026-11-21)):
- **Mac Card after a helper kill (fixed):** the Card stays pending, but its buttons stay disabled ("Review is not synchronized") until Reload Review, once for over 60 s; the chat's Card shows dimmed buttons with no reason. Cause: `LiveChatModel` reloaded review state on reconnect only while a flag was set, and applying the first replayed event cleared that flag; confirming that event reported "syncing" again, which marked the state stale, so the reload at "connected" was skipped. It happened on every reconnect that replays two or more events; the 60 s was only how long the tester waited.
- **Mac Quit with a pending Card (fixed):** Quit cancels the call, the Card and the turn; after relaunch the turn shows no reply and no Stopped or Canceled mark, and the next request carries the canceled call with no note. Cause: `closeThread(quiescing: true)` aborted every resumed conversation, including one waiting on a Card, and `beforeTool`'s catch then canceled the approval with `call_aborted`; a canceled turn whose only reply was a tool call got no bot row in `chats.history` and no `[stopped]` note in the next request.
- **`python_run` under heavy load (fixed):** the interpreter misses its own 120 s start limit, and the result reads as the cell's wall-time limit, so "never started" and "ran too long" look the same. Cause: `PyodideHost` used one deadline helper, which always ended with `.timedOut`, for the realm start and for the cell. The cell's limit was already measured from when the cell is dispatched, not from the start of the call.
- **iOS:** the tool's output is visible only to the model; the Card still reads "execution not confirmed" after a run; the Card sits below the reply it allowed; the floating header covers transcript text and the top of Cards; the accessibility issues above; on iPad, while the keyboard closes, "Waiting for your decision" is briefly drawn over the Card's buttons.
- **Mac:** two missing SF Symbols on macOS 27 (`person.crop.circle.badge.gearshape` for Edit Bot, `key.badge.plus` for Add Model Key); Browser Review wording (the Proof tab says no gateway exists; a load outside the installed route is reported as before the network boundary; the site-grant fields reset on tab switch).
- **Store:** SQLite logs "vnode unlinked while in use" for Pi `-shm` files, each time in the same second as a Quit or a Move to Trash; the stores pass `integrity_check`.
- **Model behaviour:** `qwen3:8b` sometimes invents tool results or answers without calling the tool; the app records what happened.

<a id="fixes-2026-11-20-round2"></a>
### Fixes of 2026-11-20 (round 2 defects)

**Scope.** The first three findings of the [round 2 app test](#app-tests-2026-11-20). Development work; no gate row changes.

- **Card buttons after a helper kill.** [`LiveApprovalModel`](../Packages/MemoshiApp/Sources/AppCore/LiveApprovalModel.swift) reloads its snapshot by itself, once, when it goes from disconnected to connected with a stale state; a failed reload keeps its error and Reload Review and does not loop. `LiveChatModel`'s flag and its reload are removed. While review is not synchronized, every pending Card on the Mac (chat and Waiting for You) and on the iPhone says why its buttons are disabled: not connected, reloading, or not synchronized. Tests: `LiveApprovalModelTests` (`reconnectResynchronizes`, `failedResynchronizationDoesNotLoop`, `synchronizationNotice`, `canceledRefreshKeepsLaterResynchronization`).
- **Quit keeps a pending Card (behaviour change).** A graceful close (Quit of the app-owned helper by SIGTERM, `system.shutdown`, `InProcessEngine.close`) no longer cancels a turn that waits on an approval, as [object-model.md §5.2](specs/object-model.md#52-task), [policy.md §10.1](specs/policy.md#101-lifecycle) and [04-architecture §2.3](plan/04-architecture.md#23-lifecycle) and [§2.5](plan/04-architecture.md#25-updates) already say: no spec line has a normal Quit cancel a pending approval. At close no `approval.canceled`, `card.canceled`, `tool.completed {aborted}` or `task.finished` is written, and Pi writes the tool task back as pending. At the next open the Store records `task.resumed {by: system}`, `beforeTool` reruns, writes a second `policy.decided` (ask) and waits on the same approval and Card, as after a kill. An approval past its deadline is expired at reopen. User Stop, the kill switch and a revoked grant still cancel; a reply streaming at close is still stopped. Tests: the new `RuntimeClosePendingApprovalTests`; `RuntimePolicySafetyTests` "Normal runtime close cancels its pending call and card without an effect" is now "A normal close keeps its pending call and Card for the next open; no effect runs"; `RuntimeToolAuditTests` "Normal abort or close audits both an executing parent and its approval-pending child" is split into "A normal abort audits both…" and "A normal close audits no end for an executing parent or its approval-pending child".
- **Stop with a pending Card.** The Card is still canceled. A canceled turn whose replies all called tools now has a bot row in `chats.history` (empty text, `stopReason: aborted`), and the apps show its "Stopped" caption. The next request carries `stoppedTurnNote` after the turn's last tool result ([protocol.md, `chats.abort`](specs/protocol.md#current-text-chat-profile)).
- **`python_run` start limit.** A start that misses its limit reports `PyodideFailure.startTimedOut`: "Python could not start within its start limit, so the cell did not run and nothing ran. Its interpreter was discarded; the call may be retried." The log line is `python_run failed: startTimedOut`, and Python Review's state reads `failed:startTimedOut`. The 120 s limit is unchanged. Tests: `PyodideRealmLifetimeTests` (`missedStartLimitIsNotCellWallTime`, `cellPastItsLimitReportsWallTime`, `cellLimitCountsFromCellStart`).
- **Live check on the Mac** (signed Debug app with `-MemoshiDevelopmentApprovalTool YES`, local `qwen3:8b` through the logging proxy, [evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-20-memoshi-card-fixes/)):
  - Kill of `memoshid` with a pending Card: the helper restarted, the Store recorded `task.resumed {by: system}` and a second `policy.decided`, Allow once was enabled within 3 s without Reload Review, Allow once wrote exactly one note line, and the reply showed "Interrupted, then continued after a restart" (`01-after-kill-allow.png`).
  - Quit with a pending Card, then relaunch: the Card read "Pending, expires in 30 minutes" with its buttons enabled; Allow once wrote exactly one line; the reply was marked continued after a restart (`02-after-quit-allow.png`).
  - Stop with a pending Card: the Card read "Canceled · not authorized" and the turn showed a bot bubble with the value "Stopped". The next request was the user's note request, the assistant's tool call, the tool result `[error] Tool development_note was aborted`, the assistant's `[stopped] The user stopped this turn before it finished. Answer the user's latest message, and call a tool again if that message needs one.`, and the user's "What is 7 times 8?"; the reply was "56" (`03-stop-with-card.png`, `mac-stop-requests.jsonl`).
- **iPhone** (Simulator, iPhone 14 Pro, iOS 27.0, Debug with `-MemoshiDevelopmentApprovalTool YES`, local `qwen3:8b`; [evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-20-memoshi-card-fixes/README.md)): the app was terminated with a Card pending; after relaunch the same Card was pending with its buttons enabled, Allow once ran the tool once (one note line; Store `task.resumed {by: system}`, then `approval.resolved`, one `tool.completed`). The model then called the tool a second time for the same note, so the test's wait for the turn to end timed out and its next send failed (the XCUITest reported a failure for this reason); Stop canceled that second Card, the turn was marked "Stopped, Interrupted, then continued after a restart", and "What is 7 times 8?" was answered "56".
- **Canonical CI.** [canonical-ci-21](../../evidence/2026-11-13-memoshi-canonical-s2/canonical-ci-21/README.md) passed on `fbf88f9` with a clean tree: 17 of 17 steps in 951 s; 1,506 Swift Testing tests in 16 runs plus 6 XCTest tests. [canonical-ci-20](../../evidence/2026-11-13-memoshi-canonical-s2/canonical-ci-20/README.md) on the same commit failed three `PyodideRealmLifetimeTests` realm starts (`.startTimedOut` after 61–65 s) at a 5-minute load of 94 from other sessions, the pattern of canonical-ci-14; it is kept as invalid.

**Still open:** in the test fixture a nested file-write call reruns as `grant.missing` after a reopen, because the test folder grant's binding does not survive it (the same after a crash); the 0.5–2.6 s first-cell cost outside the Pyodide worker's own timing was not investigated; the start timeout has not been seen in the app since the fix; the Mac and iPhone app sources got test targets on 2026-11-21 ([work of 2026-11-21](#work-2026-11-21)). Settled by [D139](#d139-2026-11-20): a reply streaming at Quit was stopped (canceled) by the close; a foreground subagent child waiting on a Card was aborted at close; an expired approval gave the model "denied, or its approval expired or was canceled"; a start timeout made `python_run` wait up to 120 s (the limit stays; Stop ends a start at once); the Stopped bubble sat above the canceled Card it belongs to.

<a id="d139-2026-11-20"></a>
### D139 of 2026-11-20: closing keeps work

**Scope.** The owner's choices on the round 2 open items above ([D139](plan/decisions.md#d139)). Development work; no gate row changes.

- **A close keeps running work.** `closeThread` ([`RuntimeAdapter+Threads.swift`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/RuntimeAdapter+Threads.swift)) no longer aborts a conversation at a graceful close (Quit of the app-owned helper, `system.shutdown`, `InProcessEngine.close`) and no longer waits for it to go idle; Pi's close writes running work back as pending, and the next open continues it as after a kill. At close no `task.finished` (canceled) is written, approvals, Cards, child tasks and queued inputs stay, and no `[stopped]` note follows, because the turn is not canceled. At the next open the Store records `task.resumed {by: system}`, Pi regenerates a reply that was streaming, and `chats.history` flags it `resumedAfterInterruption`. An idle close still waits for idle and projects the conversation. `waitsOnDecision` is removed. Stop is unchanged.
- **A foreground child keeps its Card.** A child waiting on a Card at close keeps the same Card; the child and its parent continue after the next open. `projectRuntimeSubagent` ([`Store+RuntimeTasks.swift`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Store/Store+RuntimeTasks.swift)) keeps a child `waiting/approval` while its approval is pending, even when Pi shows its task as pending after a close or restart; before, the reopen showed the child `queued` with its Card still pending.
- **Each approval outcome has its own result.** `refusal(_:)` in [`PolicyHooks.swift`](../Packages/MemoshiCore/Sources/Runtime/PolicyHooks.swift) gives the model, as the `beforeTool` block reason: denied "The user denied the call; nothing was done.", expired "The user did not answer in time; nothing was done.", canceled "The approval was canceled before an answer; nothing was done." A pending or unknown status keeps "Policy could not safely authorize this call. Nothing was done."
- **Stop during a Python start.** Stop already ended a start within 1 s before the change: `python_run` passes its cancellation into the start through `runCancellable`, and Python Review's Stop and Close end it at once. The 120 s start limit stays until the S6 physical-device start times set the real one. Two faults the new tests found are fixed in [`PyodideHost.swift`](../Packages/MemoshiApp/Sources/PyodideSpike/PyodideHost.swift): a start in a call already stopped ran the payload check and left the host `failed(.cancelled)` (now it throws `.cancelled` before touching state, and the host stays `idle`), and Close during the payload check reported `.cancelled` instead of `.hostClosed`.
- **End state after the Cards.** Both apps order the transcript through the new [`TranscriptLayout.swift`](../Packages/MemoshiApp/Sources/AppCore/TranscriptLayout.swift) in AppCore. A reply that has Cards and an end state (Stopped, Response failed, or Interrupted · continued after a restart) shows its bubble without the state, then its Cards, then a mark with the state after the last Card; a reply with no text gets no bubble. The mark is one VoiceOver element with identifier `chat.turnState.<messageId>`, the sender's name as label ("Assistant" on the iPhone) and the state and restart note as value; the bubble then has no value, so the state is read once. Replies without Cards and a reply still streaming are unchanged. No new text; only two translator comments in the String Catalog changed.
- **Tests.** New: `RuntimeCloseStreamingReplyTests` (`closeMidReplyContinuesAfterReopen`, `inProcessCloseMidReplyContinuesAfterRelaunch`), `RuntimeApprovalOutcomeTests.eachOutcomeHasItsOwnResult`, `RuntimeClosePendingApprovalTests.foregroundChildKeepsItsCardAcrossAClose`, `PyodideRealmLifetimeTests` `stopDuringStartEndsItAtOnce` (9 cases: where the Stop lands × tool Stop, Review Stop, Review Close) and `stoppedCallStartsNoRealm`, and `TranscriptLayoutTests` (7 tests). Renamed: `RuntimeSubagentTests.refusedFrameWhileStoppingKeepsABackgroundChild` is now `refusedFrameWhileStoppingKeepsTheWork` (the root and a background child both continue after reopen and end `done`). Updated: `RuntimeClosePendingApprovalTests.deadlinePassedWhileClosed` expects the expired text. The implementing agents reported, each `--no-parallel`: MemoshiStoreTests 117, MemoshiRuntimeTests 309, MemoshiAppTests 283 and PyodideSpikeTests 33 tests passed; the Mac and iOS Debug builds exit 0; the MemoshiDaemon and qualification targets were not run.
- **Live check on the Mac** (signed Debug app with `-MemoshiDevelopmentApprovalTool YES`, local `qwen3:8b` through the logging proxy, [evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-20-memoshi-d139/)):
  - Quit while the reply to "Write a 400-word essay about rivers." was streaming: the helper was gone; after relaunch the turn was regenerated (two model requests, the second about 12 s after the first) and finished, its reply's value read "Interrupted, then continued after a restart", and no new `[stopped]` note followed that turn (`01-quit-mid-stream-continued.png`). The request log of this check was overwritten before it was copied (`01-requests.jsonl` is empty); the two requests are from the session's own record.
  - Deny: the next request carried `<harness>[error] Tool call blocked: The user denied the call; nothing was done.</harness>`, and the model replied that the note could not be saved because the user denied the call (`02-deny-requests.jsonl`).
  - Stop with a pending Card: the accessibility order was the user message, then `approval.card.<id>` ("Canceled · not authorized"), then `chat.turnState.<messageId>` with the value "Stopped"; no empty bubble (`03-stop-card-order.png`).
- **iPhone** (Simulator, iOS 27.0, Debug, `qwen3:8b`; [evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-20-memoshi-d139/README.md)): a Card pending when the app was terminated was pending after relaunch and Allow once ran the tool once; Stop with the next Card put the turn's mark (`chat.turnState.<id>`, "Stopped") below its Card, and the next question was answered. The run through the logging proxy passed (57 s). An earlier run failed one assertion because the model called the tool again twice after it had succeeded; the proxied run's request after the relaunch carried no added note (system, user, tool call, tool result), so that repeat was the model's own choice.
- **Canonical CI.** [canonical-ci-24](../../evidence/2026-11-13-memoshi-canonical-s2/canonical-ci-24/README.md) passed on `18683b6` (D139 `9ff66ed` plus the XcodeGen 2.48.0 baseline) with a clean tree: 17 of 17 steps in 1,036 s; 1,519 Swift Testing tests in 16 runs plus 6 XCTest tests. Before it, [canonical-ci-22](../../evidence/2026-11-13-memoshi-canonical-s2/canonical-ci-22/README.md) stopped at the toolchain step because Homebrew had replaced XcodeGen 2.47.0 with 2.48.0 that morning (the baseline moved to 2.48.0 by D97's procedure in `18683b6`; canonical-ci-24 qualifies it), and [canonical-ci-23](../../evidence/2026-11-13-memoshi-canonical-s2/canonical-ci-23/README.md) failed one Pyodide realm start (`.startTimedOut` after 64 s); the Pyodide target alone then passed 33 of 33 at load 3. Both are kept as invalid.

**Still open:** a close projects nothing at close; the next open's reconcile does, as after a crash, so a turn Pi settled just before Quit is written as finished at the next open. A foreground child's continuation depends on Pi accepting the rerun subagent tool's re-submission with the same request ID (the same path as after a kill; the child test shows it working). A close can cost a second generation at relaunch (paid tokens on a cloud model). The Pyodide payload check before the web view exists does not react to cancellation; it takes under 1 s here, its device cost is untested. Pyodide tests that used a 60 s start limit failed with `startTimedOut` when other sessions loaded the machine heavily (loads of about 50–105); since 2026-11-21 they use a test-only 300 s limit ([work of 2026-11-21](#work-2026-11-21)). "Response failed" also moves after the Cards, though D139 names only Stopped and Interrupted · continued. Placing each Card at its tool call within the reply is left to the Phase 1 transcript design; no UI test uses `chat.turnState.<id>` yet ([remaining work §1.4](plan/remaining-work.md#14-engineering-owed-before-or-at-the-gate)).

<a id="work-2026-11-21"></a>
### Work of 2026-11-21: the no-owner engineering items

**Scope.** The items of [remaining work §1.4](plan/remaining-work.md#14-engineering-owed-before-or-at-the-gate) that needed no owner input, committed in `7f766c8` and pushed. Development work and evidence on the development Mac and the iOS Simulator, under load from other sessions; no gate row changes.

- **Transport.**
  - Send queue ([protocol.md §11](specs/protocol.md#11-backpressure-and-limits)): [`EventSendQueue`](../Packages/MemoshiDaemon/Sources/MemoshiDaemon/EventSendQueue.swift) replaces the session's own pending state. When a frame would pass 10,000 frames or 8 MiB, it first concatenates the pending `message.delta` frames of one message and channel that continue each other, never across a durable event, never a frame already being written, and within the 64 KiB event payload limit; then it drops the oldest UI-only frames. Durable events and `system.resync_required` are never merged or dropped; only durable frames alone still close the connection with 4429. `tool.output`, `codemode.output` and `terminal.output` are not frame types yet, so no output window is replaced. Tests: `EventSendQueueTests` (9) and three `RPCSessionTests` at the real limits; the merge and displace tests failed on the old code (closed with 4429).
  - Peer check ([protocol.md §2.2](specs/protocol.md#22-websocket-over-the-unix-socket)): the part that reads the connected descriptor stays on the event loop, before any handler is installed; `SecCodeCheckValidity` runs on a concurrent queue off the loop; the audit token never leaves C. Closing the listener waits for checks in progress, and `LocalConnection.revalidatePeer` uses the same split. `slowPeerCheckDoesNotBlockOtherConnections` failed with `.timedOut` on the old seam; it and `listenerCloseWaitsForAPeerCheck` pass.
  - DaemonSupervisor: the wait for the agent's daemon after Disable and the status line during waits ([Mac supervisor row](#foundation-implementation-proof)); 9 logic tests, with no red run because the old logic sat in the view.
  - The MemoshiIPC, MemoshiClient and MemoshiDaemon targets pass in full with `--no-parallel`.
- **App test targets and app core.** New unit-test targets `MemoshiTests` (Mac) and `MemoshiMobileTests` (iPhone/iPad Simulator): not hosted, so no app or memoshid is launched, and each compiles its app's own sources. [`scripts/test-apps.sh`](../scripts/test-apps.sh) creates and deletes its own simulator and is CI's new "App tests" step. The Mac app says when a replaced credential is still saved and tries to remove it again each time Model Settings open; a credential a provider uses again is never removed. A listing refused as busy waits for the earlier attachment's settings work to return, then asks once more (the new test failed before the fix). No app constructs the device transport or mints message IDs yet; `DeviceMessageIDSourceTests` fails if an app source ever calls `DeviceULID.next()`. A Run again that found no wait now tells the model "Tool X may have partially run; the user chose Run again, but the call had already ended, so it did not run again." (`ResumeNoteTests`; the Store test failed before the fix; tested at the Store and note level only, because no public path produces it in a runtime test). MemoshiAppTests, MemoshiStoreTests and MemoshiRuntimeTests pass in full. Not covered by the new targets: `LiveChatModel`'s reconnect and resumed-flag handling, ShellFeature's layout values.
- **Apps UI** ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-21-memoshi-apps-ui/README.md)).
  - The XCUITest accessibility audit, before (`0680cf2`) and after, issues at Large and at XXXL: iPhone 78 → 53 and 54 → 43; iPad 118 → 97 and 86 → 90. On iPad XXXL a second run gave 107 for the before app and 86 for the after app, so that difference is within the audit's run-to-run spread.
  - Changes: `DesignFittingStack` (DesignSystem) replaces `ViewThatFits` for the Approval Card, resume choice and Python Review buttons; the Development Review sheets get the palette and checked tint (`.previewAppearance()` outermost); section headers and footers use the checked secondary text; the endpoint field wraps; the "Apple design guidelines" link has a 44 pt tap area on iOS; Edit Bot uses `person.badge.gearshape` and Add Model Key `plus` (the old names do not exist on macOS 27.0.1 or iOS 27.0).
  - Header: `DesignControlRoles.barBacking`, the opaque `surface.window`, sits behind the iPhone/iPad conversation header up to the top edge, the inspector's header and the live composer down to the bottom edge; the pill, toolbar and tab bar keep their glass. Transcript text no longer shows through or around the pill (portrait and landscape screenshots, before and after).
  - DesignSystemTests (34) and MemoshiAppTests (297) pass; the boundaries lint passes. Left: the pill's text-size cap and the "Engine on this device" contrast flag (owner questions below); the Reading Note card's text, flagged for contrast in every run before and after, cause not found; system bar buttons, the search field and iPad tab bar items; disabled buttons; the landscape keyboard overlap.
- **Sandbox.**
  - S4-T7 at load 5.5 (the other sessions were waiting on the build lock), n 100 per bot: sandboxed exec p95 9.18 ms for 1 bot and 10.28 ms for 4 bots at once, against the unsandboxed `LocalExecutionEnv` path (`RuntimeComputerHandle.unsandboxedBaseline(cwd:)`) at 1.52 ms alone and 1.54 ms as four streams; cold start 1 bot p50 25.28 ms, p95 65.17 ms, 4 bots p50 34.68 ms, p95 291.91 ms ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-21-memoshi-s4-t7-four/README.md)). The cold start is load-sensitive (p50 289 ms at load 23–50 on 2026-11-19); which phase dominates is not recorded.
  - S4-T10: the probes keep their outcome under `MEMOSHI_ENV_GATEWAY_EVIDENCE`. The stand-in memoshid socket accepted outside and got `EPERM` inside; `docker.sock` refused outside (ECONNREFUSED) and got `EPERM` inside. The Docker case applies only for 0 or ECONNREFUSED outside; without a socket the test cancels itself as not applicable.
  - Attribution: U+0600 reaches the reader as raw UTF-8. A new defect, fixed test-first: the kernel cuts a report at 1,008 bytes, and a cut inside a multi-byte character makes `log stream` deliver only `<compose failure [illegible log arguments]>`, which the reader's filter dropped, so the report disappeared without a gap; it is now counted in `report_truncated` without text.
  - Removal ACL: no bot can reach the window before it (the sandbox never maps the live source folder); a new test with a barrier there shows an outside file kept and reported. Coverage of existing behaviour, not a fix.
  - Listings: the new `SandboxListingGuard` leaves protected names out of `listFiles`, `opendir`/`readdir` and watch changes (red: 9 failures, secrets listed). Ignore files below a listed directory are still read without a decision.
  - MemoshiRuntimeTests: 316 tests in 57 suites pass with `--no-parallel`.
- **Browser** ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-21-memoshi-browser-seam/README.md); every S3 run is a `--only` development run on the uncommitted tree, never a complete control).
  - Gateway seam: `BrowserReviewGateway` and `BrowserGatewayStarter` let the harness inject the product `BrowserGateway` on a test-hooked `EgressGateway`. The rewritten `product-separate-profile-gateway` scenario runs six tasks over owned `.test` hosts on the gateway route (same-site link, the other site and back, confirmed POST, ungranted form, replayed form, a refused separate opening); all six succeed. WebKit sends no `Sec-Fetch-Site` on that plain-http route, so the analyzer accepts it absent there only.
  - Frame rule: `BrowserAuthorization.dependsOnPrivateData` and `BrowserSessionReview.holdsPrivateData()` read the profile's store and keep the flag set once cookies are seen. A separate profile whose own page sets a cookie now counts as holding private data, so its forms ask for confirmation; the new edges check refuses its frame of another granted origin.
  - T9 (S3 06 row 8): a handle to a cross-origin frame's `window` arrives as `undefined` in the parent frame's isolated world; `BrowserFrameTargeting` now uses the iframe element and re-checks its document on every geometry read. Lockdown was not involved. The Lockdown suite ran 8 of 8.
  - F5: a click waits at most 1 s for its navigation's decision and 2 s more for an allowed navigation to start (`BrowserNavigationProgress`); the refused redirect opening now reports `controlRefusedSeparateGateway` (26.8 ms; before, 4.5 ms with no code). Click steps take longer (a same-site click 9.5 → 196 ms).
  - F3: a return's empty document that had started loading before a cancel was later judged an ordinary move and opened a new separate profile after the task. A cancel now stops that load (`webView.stopLoading()`), `decide` refuses anything else while the document loads or shows (`separateProfileReturnStopped`), and `BrowserTaskRunner.cancel()` stops profile changes at once. The edges scenario passed twice. Remaining risk [INFERENCE]: a commit already on its way when the cancel lands.
  - Harness: the three fail-closed rows are judged from what they record (the `.local` row needs exactly `egress.invalidRule`); both sinks are probed after the driver exits (`liveness.json`, `sinksAlive`, 20 of 20 alive); `noSeparateAfterCancel`; `--only`. Older recorded runs now re-analyse as not passing, because they have no `liveness.json`.
  - Browser Review: the Proof tab says the gateway route runs but does not prove that nothing bypasses it; a load outside the granted sites has its own message; the site-grant fields survive tab switches (`BrowserSiteGrantDraft`).
  - AppCore `--filter Browser` 104 tests and the harness's 79 pass; the Mac and iOS apps build.
- **Tooling.** `build-memoshi-mls.sh` stages inside `Artifacts/`, checks the bindings, `Info.plist` and every `Headers` folder, and installs by same-volume renames; `payload-manifest.json` is removed before the first rename and rewritten after the last, so an install stopped midway leaves no manifest and `memoshi-mls-payload-manifest.py --check` refuses the payload (3 new tests, red then green). Load relays run with `--no-grant-log` leave the grant counts out; the driver and `check_d123_v2.py` require them from the main relay only. S2's `refusingAuthority`: the driver was right; the fixture used one time for the lease expiry and the revocation, and `renewalsAreSplitAtTheReportedRemoval` now tells them apart. Pyodide tests that do not measure the start use a test-only 300 s limit through the internal start entry; the product's 120 s, its 1–180 s range and the explicit start-limit tests are unchanged. The Pyodide target passed 33 of 33 at load about 4.5, so the 300 s limit is not yet shown under heavy load. The two 2026-11-16 evidence READMEs end with a dated section holding the facts from their session-local notes.
- **vnode messages** ([Pi_Swift report](../../evidence/2026-11-21-memoshi-vnode/pi-swift-report.md)). The "vnode unlinked while in use" lines on Pi `-shm` files come from Pi_Swift: `NativeSqliteDatabase` turns persistent WAL off, so the last close deletes the `-shm`, and Apple's libsqlite3 reports that delete before it cancels its own watch. An `unlink` interposer showed every flagged file deleted by `NativeSqliteDatabase.close` in the same process; a C harness with Pi's open/close sequence logged it on 174 of 200 closes with persistent WAL off and 0 of 200 with it on. Nothing in Memoshi's Trash or Quit path deletes these files, so Memoshi has no fix; every affected store passed `integrity_check`. One `memoshi.sqlite-shm` occurrence in the system log was not investigated.
- **Canonical CI.** [canonical-ci-25](../../evidence/2026-11-13-memoshi-canonical-s2/canonical-ci-25/README.md) passed on `7f766c8` with a clean tree: 18 of 18 steps in 1,065 s, the App tests step 83 s; 1,562 Swift Testing tests in 16 runs, 111 release-tooling tests, spike self-tests 79 + 2. Not covered: the opt-in S4-T7 measurement, the signed replay run, the sharded S1.17 runner, the full S2 run and the full S3 no-bypass control.

Every product scenario had 0 sink events, among them `product-separate-profile-gateway` (S3 06 row 9: the six gateway tasks; the ungranted and replayed forms refused) and `product-lockdown-suite` (8 of 8, T9's `trusted-cross-origin-frame` included, 06 row 8). The 1-minute load was 29.5 at the start and up to 482.6 from other sessions; free disk 20–38 GB. This is the first complete Mac `subagent` batch, and on the `slice-2` pin it counts under D90. The iPhone `IOSSmoke` UI test passed in 563 s ([log](../../evidence/2026-11-21-memoshi-app-recheck/ios-run/xcodebuild-test-filtered.log)) [INFERENCE: in the iOS Simulator, the 2026-11-20 Simulator harness]. In it, Python Review's interpreter missed its start limit, and the step showed the new `startTimedOut` text ("Python could not start within its start limit, so the cell did not run…"). On the Mac (`steps.txt`), a reply, Stop and a helper kill mid-reply (left "Stopped") worked.
- **Mac sends after Stop and the vanished window** ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-21-memoshi-mac-poststop/README.md)): neither is an app defect, and no code changed. The person at the Mac had switched the LG display to another Space, and the AX test driver cannot see a window on a Space that is not showing. The test script did not check the driver's `not found` results, so the sends looked lost. With the window on a showing Space, 7 of 7 sends made 0.5–13 s after Stop were delivered and answered. The window comes back through Open Memoshi in the menu bar extra, the Dock's reopen and Window ▸ Memoshi.

**Questions for the owner** ([remaining work §1.1](plan/remaining-work.md#11-owner-decisions), rows 19–27):
- **D130's 120 s for a kickstarted LaunchAgent.** After `launchctl kickstart` of an enabled agent the app waits 3 s for its daemon; its own helper gets 120 s ([D130](plan/decisions.md#d130): "the Mac app waits up to 120 s for its helper"). (a) The 120 s also applies to the agent, reading "its helper" as the bundled memoshid however it was started; (b) keep 3 s, and D130 says only the app's own child gets 120 s.
- **What ends a lab separate profile.** [D136](plan/decisions.md#d136) discards it "when the task ends", and lab browsing has no task. Today it ends when a typed address opens, the lab page loads, permissions are revoked or re-authorized, or the session closes. (a) Keep that and state it in the S3 report; (b) an explicit "Discard separate profile" control; (c) an idle timeout; (d) Hand Back also ends it.
- **Header pill text size.** The pill is capped at the largest standard text size, with Large Content Viewer instead (`61ba644`), and the audit flags that cap for Dynamic Type on every chat screen (3 issues each). Keep the cap and accept the flag, or let the header grow at accessibility sizes.
- **"Engine on this device" contrast.** On the new opaque backing the pill's subtitle (`text.secondary`) was flagged for contrast on one chat screen in 3 of the 5 after runs, never before; keep its colour or change it.
- **Landscape with the keyboard up.** The tab bar covers the composer and Palette covers Send, in the builds before and after this work; fixing it needs a layout decision for the short landscape height (with [§1.1 row 18](plan/remaining-work.md#11-owner-decisions)'s transcript space).
- **Rows 24–27, from Pi_Swift:** the pin move to `slice-2.2`, Code Mode on iOS and WasmKit, the per-API freeze list, and E9b and E28 at the gate ([Pi_Swift exchange of 2026-11-21](#pi-swift-2026-11-21)).

**Still open:** the daemon acting on `system.cancel`; reaping detached `setsid` children; F3's residual race [INFERENCE]; S3 06 rows 8 and 9 on the reference box, and a real cross-site frame for row 8; S4-T7 and S4-T10 on the reference box and against live sockets; S1.17, where the only failure left is Pi_Swift's `HarnessToolsTests` test (a `slice-2` defect fixed in `slice-2.1`, [below](#pi-swift-2026-11-21)), but the suite is not in CI and has had no quiet-machine or reference-box run; S1 kill/resume, where the Mac `subagent` batch is complete and the nested Code Mode and compaction points, iOS and the reference box are not; the Python start the iPhone smoke run missed (cause not recorded); the replay, canonical CI and a full S2 run on the gate commit; `BrowserNetworkBoundaryTests` with Proton VPN connected (needs the owner); the Reading Note card contrast flags; the app-target gaps above ([remaining work §1.3](plan/remaining-work.md#13-runs-owed-that-need-no-owner-input), [§1.4](plan/remaining-work.md#14-engineering-owed-before-or-at-the-gate)).

<a id="pi-swift-2026-11-21"></a>
### Pi_Swift exchange of 2026-11-21

**Scope.** Documents only: no Memoshi code, pin or decision changed. Memoshi filed its state and requests R1–R7 for Pi_Swift's agent at `~/Documents/memoshi/Pi_Swift/docs/requests/2026-11-21-memoshi-state-and-requests.md`, with its answers to Pi_Swift's 2026-11-20 handoff and to the "Needs Memoshi" rows of Pi_Swift's state note (`~/Documents/memoshi/handoffs/2026-11-20-pi-swift-state-for-memoshi.md`). Pi_Swift answered in the same file the same day (Pi_Swift `main` `5648883`, now `ed736e4`; pointer note `~/Documents/memoshi/handoffs/2026-11-20-pi-swift-answers-to-memoshi-requests.md`). No Pi_Swift engine code changed, so `slice-2.2` (`56d6fc8`) is still the tag to pin. Recorded in [remaining work §1.1](plan/remaining-work.md#11-owner-decisions), [§1.4](plan/remaining-work.md#14-engineering-owed-before-or-at-the-gate) and [§1.5](plan/remaining-work.md#15-pi_swift-dependencies-its-own-repository-and-gate-report-tue-2026-11-24).

- **Four contracts Pi_Swift now keeps** (a change gets a migration note in its `docs/CHANGES-FOR-CONSUMERS.md` first), with Memoshi's compliance:
  - A graceful close keeps running work and approval waits ([D139](plan/decisions.md#d139)); new Pi_Swift test `MemoshiContractTests.approvalAcrossGracefulClose`, 10/10.
  - A subagent resumes when re-submitted with the same request ID, derived from the task's own ID. Memoshi complies: `RuntimeSubagents.swift` uses `subagent:<api.taskId>` for a foreground child and `subagent:<anchor task id>` for a background one.
  - `task.resumed` may be recorded at first open with read-only calls only, because any `submit` or `wait…` starts the scheduler. Memoshi complies: `recordResumedTurns` runs before the first resume.
  - `contextView()` lists aborted entries, but without `at:` it reads the latest tail, while a request's messages are cut at the request's cut-off. Open: the stopped-reply workaround calls `contextView(context:)` without `at:` (`RuntimeAdapter+StoppedReplies.swift:51`).
- **Answers.** R1 (aborted replies dropped from the model's context): declined before the gate, because upstream does the same; a Swift-only field for why a partial ended is planned after the gate (Pi_Swift R-89), and Memoshi's workaround stays supported. R2 (the vnode messages): diagnosis accepted, closing the gate part of Pi_Swift's P04-12; the fix waits until after the gate (R-90), because persistent WAL breaks 12 file-layout tests; no store damage observed. R3 (`HarnessToolsTests` "settles details()…"): a real ordering defect in `slice-2`, fixed in `slice-2.1` by `18975a4`; with `slice-2`'s `ToolAPI.swift` 45 and 183 of 1,000 repetitions failed (with and without added CPU load), 0 of 2,000 with the fix. R4 (`runCommand` pipes): mostly met; a silent leftover costs at most about 100 ms, also after a kill, abort or timeout; one that keeps writing is waited on, as upstream does; a cap after the gate. R5 (discovery-globals hook): waits on owner decision 7. R6 (H1 for the nested Code Mode kill point): met; in `MemoshiContractTests.codemodeNestedInterruption` a replay-unsafe nested call killed between its intent and its result waits on `pi_swift.interrupted/1`, and Skip, Mark as done and Run again each let the script finish with the effect once. Memoshi's own nested Code Mode kill point can now be built and is owed. R7 (strict oracle): the setup is in Pi_Swift `docs/VERIFICATION.md` §6; without strict mode the oracle tests skip and are not cross-runtime evidence.
- **`slice-2.2`.** An annotated tag on `56d6fc8` (2026-11-20), qualified by Pi_Swift CI run 44730758520 with all six lanes green. It holds `slice-2.1` (`ae98216`: the 2026-11-18 deep review's fixes and R3's fix) and the iOS private-container check (`6c6144f`). One source break for Memoshi: `PropertyMap.init(ordered:)` is removed. It can be pinned by revision, without the `../Pi_Swift-slice-2` path checkout. Because R3 is fixed in `slice-2.1`, the move clears S1.17's last failure. Memoshi stays on `slice-2` until the owner decides row 24 (pin moves are decisions, [D134](plan/decisions.md#d134)).
- **Device results** (Pi_Swift's own Release builds and harness, development-signed in team `476T8NQCSX`; not Memoshi's app). The owner released the devices for these runs on 2026-11-20 and registered the iPad in that team, so it can run development builds. iPhone 14 Pro (iOS 27.2.1): swipe-away during a continued-processing turn 10/10; a second open refused 10 + 10 (`StoreWriterConflict`); background with the store closed 10/10; lock 10/10; no 0xdead10cc; 50 kills 50/50 with 0 duplicates; a pending approval across a kill 10/10 ([evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-20-pi-swift-device/README.md)). Code Mode on the iPhone and the iPad Pro 12.9-inch (iPadOS 27.0.1, [evidence](../../evidence/2026-11-20-pi-swift-ipad/)): wasm2c meets every D98 limit on both (iPad: W1 62 / 69 ms, W2 143 / 148 ms, W3c 2.3 MB retained); WasmKit misses W1 warm (about 308 ms against 100) and W3c (2.4 GB retained) on both, filed upstream as [swiftwasm/WasmKit#536](https://github.com/swiftwasm/WasmKit/issues/536). Memoshi passes its own QuickJS loader (`RuntimeExtensionSession.swift:120`), which selects WasmKit, so S1's Code Mode row stays fail ([S1 §3](research/spikes/S1-runtime.md#3-outcome-table)). None of this is a Memoshi device row.
- **New owner rows** ([remaining work §1.1](plan/remaining-work.md#11-owner-decisions)): 24, move the pin to `slice-2.2`; 25, Code Mode on iOS (the memory limit, a per-VM linear-memory cap, and dropping WasmKit as a shipping fallback); 26, the per-API freeze list for the gate line and which v1.5.0 removals Memoshi depends on (`taskGraph()`, `documentState`, `viewState`); 27, whether E9b (TLS 1.2 PSK without forward secrecy) and E28 (no host identity when a consumer bypasses `BotRuntime`) are acceptable at the gate.
- **New engineering items** ([remaining work §1.4](plan/remaining-work.md#14-engineering-owed-before-or-at-the-gate)): close the iPhone/iPad in-process engine on `scenePhase == .background` inside `beginBackgroundTask` and reopen it on active (SwiftUI apps never get `applicationDidEnterBackground`; on Pi_Swift's iPhone a store left open lost its lease while suspended, and its next commit failed the session); adopt the SDK's wasm2c default after decision 25, then rescore S1's Code Mode row; an App Intent is cancelled after about 30 s, which binds Memoshi's first Intent (none exists yet); pass `at:` with the request's cut-off in the stopped-reply workaround.

Gate rows: no count changes. S1.17's remaining failure has a fix behind row 24; S1's Code Mode row stays fail until Memoshi selects wasm2c.

### Latest research and next-version handoff

**Start here when continuing the Grok + Kuvo + Pi research request.** This section is self-contained; the receiving agent does not need the previous chat or its tool state.

#### User intent and work completed

The user wants a native Swift Mac/iPhone assistant combining **Kuvo Code's workspaces**, **Grok Bot's persistent teammates**, **Pi Durable and the relevant Pi ecosystem as the brain**, and **Federico Viticci's Apple-native resource-card direction**: Reminders, Calendar, music, Shazam and other supported platform features. They asked for roughly ten leading references, a comprehensive comparison, every supplied link checked, and a combined next version.

The completed research deliverables are **research and a proposed product/architecture specification**. That research pass built no executable or integration. The apps later gained the [design preview](specs/design-reference.md), then the [bounded text-only Mac integration](#text-chat-development-proof), not the whole proposal. Research does not itself adopt remaining features or authorize a roadmap change.

- **Comparison:** [2026-10-20 report](research/2026-10-20-comparison-report.md), including four feature matrices and licensing/security qualifications. Its [source ledger](research/2026-10-20-comparison-report.md#12-every-supplied-link-checked) retains all **21 unique URLs**; Cua's announcement appeared twice.
- **Combined version:** [NEXT_VERSION.md](plan/NEXT_VERSION.md), including a native layout schematic, architecture, typed resource views, device routing, five product journeys, dependency gates and future acceptance scenarios.
- **Authority:** Draft 4 and D1–D122 are binding, subject to explicit amendments and supersession. D44–D56 adopted the native Apple direction, the engine on every device, permissions and cards; D79 adopted live Apple-data resources and recovery; D86 adopted QuickAsk, visual views, Apple tools and shared rooms (in v1.1). D87 staged them, and D117 and D122 re-stage what D106–D116 moved; D88 amends offline acceptance; D91 permits Kuvo source/reuse, which D106 does not take for the interface; D106 sets the design lead, superseding D92 and D94 there; D95 replaces local transport; D96 selects private Mac working-copy qualification without full S4 acceptance; D97 governs stable-toolchain upgrades; D98 accepts the Code Mode workload limits; D99 picks the public-files domain; D100 routes Python's network through the sealed native bridge. D106–D122 (2026-11-13) then moved feature stages and added scope, as the [scope amendment](#scope-amendment-of-2026-11-13) above summarises; no fork is selected. The rest remains proposed; see the [adoption map](plan/NEXT_VERSION.md#12-adoption-map-changes-versus-preserved-contracts).
- **Freshness:** the [requested-source refresh](research/README.md#latest-requested-source-refresh) supersedes overlapping product facts in the older landscape and historical version snapshots below, including those taken earlier on the same date. It does not supersede accepted decisions. The existing 26-profile matrix was not regenerated.
